### [34] [An Empirical Measurement of Jailbreaking Evaluators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10594)
*Yujie Mu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文系统比较了六种越狱评估器在统一人工标注数据上的表现，发现JADES总体性能最佳，HarmBench和StrongReject也表现良好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有越狱研究通常独立验证评估器，导致重复耗费资源、结果难以横向比较，并且不同评估器对“越狱成功”的定义差异会显著影响攻击强度和研究进展的判断。因此，有必要在统一、受控的实验环境下比较常用评估器。

Method: 研究选取HarmBench、JailbreakBench、JailbreakRadar、StrongReject、JADES和JailMeter六种评估器，在JailbreakQR和JailMeter-Eva两个具有人类判断标注的数据集上进行测试，以人工判断作为参考标准。评估指标包括与人工判断的一致性、错误类型以及不同攻击族之间的稳定性。对于依赖通用大语言模型裁判的评估器，研究统一使用相同的模型骨干，以控制模型差异带来的影响。

Result: JADES取得最佳总体表现；HarmBench和StrongReject同样表现较好。研究还系统报告了各评估器与人工判断之间的一致性、具体错误类型及跨攻击族的一致性，从而揭示不同评估器可能产生不同的越狱成功判断。

Conclusion: 评估器的选择会实质性影响越狱攻击与防御研究的结论。JADES在统一人工标注基准上具有最强的综合表现，而HarmBench和StrongReject也是具有竞争力的选择。未来研究应采用标准化基准和受控评测流程，以提升结果的可比性与可复现性。

Abstract: Expert evaluation of jailbreak responses is costly and difficult to scale, so the community increasingly relies on automated evaluators to determine whether an attack succeeds. However, jailbreak studies typically validate their chosen evaluator independently, repeatedly spending resources on similar evaluation efforts while making results across papers difficult to compare. Different evaluators also encode different definitions of jailbreak success, meaning that reported attack strength and apparent progress can depend substantially on which evaluator is used. We systematically compare six evaluators that recur in recent jailbreak attack and defense research: HarmBench, JailbreakBench, JailbreakRadar, StrongReject, JADES, and JailMeter. To our knowledge, no prior study has evaluated all six on the same human-labeled data under a controlled setup. We evaluate them on JailbreakQR and JailMeter-Eva, using human judgments as the reference, and measure agreement with humans, error types, and consistency across attack families. For evaluators that require a general-purpose LLM judge, we use a shared backbone to control for model-specific variation. We found that JADES exhibits the best overall performance, while HarmBench and StrongReject also demonstrate good performance.

</details>


### [35] [Threshold Choice, Not Sample Size, Bounds Trustless Verification of Nondeterministic Compound AI Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10601)
*Alper Alimoglu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种用于跨边缘、云端和轨道节点验证复合AI流水线可复现性的协议，并通过实验表明：在相同指标与执行分布下，判定阈值比重执行次数更关键；即使增加采样次数，也无法有效阻止针对相同输入的系统性伪造。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复合AI流水线包含大语言模型、检索器和工具调用，受随机采样、模型更新及动态工具响应影响，同一输入可能产生不同输出。在跨组织节点执行且中间结果可能被丢弃的环境中，验证者还需应对节点不诚实和共享记录间歇不可访问的问题。现有方法通常只能处理其中两类挑战，缺乏同时覆盖非确定性、不诚实执行者和网络分区的统一方案。

Method: 协议为每个阶段的输入、输出及上下文提交摘要，并通过策略摘要固定评价指标和判定阈值；摘要被锚定而不依赖执行节点的可信报告，网络分区时允许延迟处理。争议判定基于k次重新执行结果的中位数，不依赖节点法定人数。实验使用合成HotpotQA流水线，比较固定阈值、基于每次执行推导的阈值以及针对该规则构造的攻击。

Result: 在校准固定阈值下，协议接受了45次诚实复现中的44次，并拒绝了105对发散结果中的104对，但在k=5时仍有29次同输入伪造中的27次被接受。增加重执行次数并未解决该问题，因为采样只会提高估计精度，不能改变系统性偏差。在保持评价指标和复现分布不变时，基于每次执行推导的阈值在k=5时没有误拒绝诚实提交，并检测出29次攻击中的19次；相同零诚实拒绝约束下的最佳固定阈值仅检测出9次。该推导规则在k=3时拒绝了15次诚实提交中的3次，并能检测出针对该规则构造的15次攻击中的11次。

Conclusion: 主要结论是，验证效果受阈值设定支配，而非简单取决于重执行样本数。中位数聚合能够缓解随机波动，却无法识别稳定的同输入伪造或系统性偏差。基于执行结果动态推导阈值具有更强检测能力，但会引入误拒绝和适应性攻击风险；由于该规则仅完成测量而未部署，其实际可靠性、攻击者模型和跨环境泛化能力仍需进一步验证。

Abstract: Compound AI pipelines chain LLM calls, retrievers, and tools and are nondeterministic: sampling, model updates, and volatile tool responses make one input yield different outputs across runs. Such pipelines increasingly run across edge, cloud, and orbital nodes owned by no single party, whose optimizations discard intermediate results before inspection. Verifying reproduction there means tolerating nondeterministic outputs, a node that may not report honestly, and intermittent access to any shared record; existing work addresses at most two at once. We give a protocol covering all three: it commits digests of each stage's inputs, outputs, and context under a policy digest pinning the metric and threshold, anchors them without trusting the executing node, defers under partition, and decides a challenge on the median of $k$ re-executions, with no quorum. Where that procedure breaks is the main result. On a synthetic HotpotQA pipeline a calibrated fixed threshold accepts 44 of 45 honest reproductions and rejects 104 of 105 divergent pairs, yet lets same-input fabrication through in 27 of 29 trials at k=5, more samples being no help since sampling sharpens an estimate without moving it. Holding that metric and this pipeline's re-execution spread fixed, the binding constraint is the threshold rather than the sample size: one derived per execution detects 19 of 29 where the best constant matched to the same zero honest rejections reaches 9, rejects no honest commitment at k=5 though 3 of 15 at k=3, and catches 11 of 15 of an attacker built against it, which has to aim at a target drawn only after its commitment exists. That rule is measured rather than deployed.

</details>


### [36] [Adaptive Diffusion Freezing: Privacy-preserving Diffusion Models Against Membership Inference Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10608)
*Jialu Guo,Xiao Han,Junjie Wu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出自适应扩散冻结（ADF）框架，通过跨时间步自适应冻结训练降低扩散模型的成员推断攻击风险，并在隐私性、生成质量与训练效率之间实现更优权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型训练容易产生数据过度记忆，从而泄露成员信息。现有隐私保护方法难以同时兼顾隐私保护效果、生成质量和计算效率，因此需要一种更精细的数据参与控制机制。

Method: ADF利用掩码矩阵控制不同数据子集在各扩散时间步中的参与程度。作者首先通过基于预训练模型的风险感知冻结策略，根据数据记忆倾向估计成员推断风险；随后抑制高风险数据子集与时间步组合的训练贡献，以减少过度记忆并使成员样本与非成员样本的模型行为更加一致。

Result: 在多个数据集上的实验表明，ADF能够有效防御成员推断攻击，并相较多种基线方法取得先进的隐私性、生成效用与训练效率综合权衡。

Conclusion: 跨时间步、风险感知的数据冻结能够有效缓解扩散模型的成员信息泄露。ADF在不显著牺牲生成质量的前提下提升隐私保护和训练效率，具有较好的实用价值。

Abstract: Diffusion models have achieved remarkable success in generative tasks across various areas, however their training process raises significant privacy concerns, particularly under membership inference attacks (MIAs). Prior studies on privacy-preserving of diffusion models fail to balance privacy, utility, and efficiency. To address this gap, we propose a novel framework of privacy-preserving diffusion models, Adaptive Diffusion Freezing (ADF), which can defend against MIAs with better trade-off. By leveraging cross-timestep adaptive freezing training, ADF explicitly control the participation of different data subsets across diffusion timesteps via a mask matrix, which reduces the over-memorization and leads to more uniform model behaviors between member and nonmember samples. To construct a freezing mask matrix that effectively reduce membership leakage without unnecessarily harming generation quality, we introduce a pretraining-based risk-aware freezing policy to estimate MIA risk based on memorization tendency, and suppress the contribution of the subset-timestep pairs with higher risk. Evaluations on multiple datasets demonstrate that ADF provides effective defense performance as well as state-of-the-art privacy-utility-efficiency trade-off performance compared to various baselines.

</details>


### [37] [Black-Box Membership Inference via Word-Level Probability Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10611)
*Shengjie Niu,Yeheng Ge,Jian Huang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出严格黑盒场景下的词级概率成员推断攻击方法WPMIA，仅依赖模型生成的文本续写来估计输入文本是否出现在训练数据中，并在多种开源及专有大语言模型上取得优于现有黑盒基线的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有成员推断攻击通常需要访问逐令牌logits或概率，但专有大语言模型通常只提供文本输出，导致这些方法难以适用。本文旨在构建一种无需内部概率信息、可用于实际专有模型隐私审计的成员推断方法。

Method: WPMIA通过蒙特卡洛采样结合局部核平滑估计词级生成概率，再将词级估计聚合为序列级似然估计。此外，方法基于不同长度或内容的前缀构造条件似然，以放大成员样本与非成员样本之间的分布差异，并据此完成成员判定。

Result: 在多种开源大语言模型上的实验表明，WPMIA持续优于现有黑盒基线。在GPT-5-Chat、Gemini-2.5-Flash和Claude-4.5-Haiku等专有模型上，平均TPR@5%FPR达到42.0，说明该方法在严格黑盒访问条件下具有较强的攻击与审计能力。

Conclusion: WPMIA为仅能返回文本续写的专有大语言模型提供了一种统计上有依据的成员推断框架，验证了严格黑盒隐私审计的可行性。其主要贡献在于将采样得到的词级概率估计与前缀条件化似然结合起来，但其效果仍可能受采样预算、核平滑参数、文本分布和模型输出策略影响。

Abstract: Membership inference attacks (MIAs) have emerged as critical tools for auditing privacy risks in large language models (LLMs), aiming to determine whether a given text was included in a model's training corpus. However, most existing MIAs require access to per-token logits or probabilities, making them inapplicable in practice to proprietary LLMs that expose only textual continuations. To address this underexplored setting, we propose Word-level Probability MIA (WPMIA), a statistically principled MIA for strict black-box privacy auditing. WPMIA estimates word-level generation probabilities via Monte Carlo sampling with local kernel smoothing, then aggregates these estimates into a sequence-level likelihood estimator. Furthermore, WPMIA constructs the likelihood conditioned on different prefixes, thereby amplifying the distributional differences between members and non-members. We evaluate WPMIA across various open-source LLMs and find that it consistently outperforms existing black-box baselines. Importantly, we also evaluate WPMIA on modern proprietary LLMs, including GPT-5-Chat, Gemini-2.5-Flash, and Claude-4.5-Haiku, achieving an average TPR@5\%FPR of 42.0 across these models. These results offer a sound foundation for future research on strict black-box membership inference. Code is available at \href{https://github.com/niusj03/WPMIA}{https://github.com/niusj03/WPMIA}.

</details>


### [38] [PEARL: A Task-Aware Framework for Evaluating Differentially Private Synthetic Educational Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10612)
*Xianghui Meng,Yujing Zhang,Jionghao Lin*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出PEARL审计框架，用于判断差分隐私合成学习者数据是否同时满足有效性、隐私性、预测有用性、公平性和目标教育任务适用性。96种实验设置中仅12种数据集通过全部适用检查，表明隐私保护并不等于任务可用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究通常将隐私保护与预测性能分开评估，无法判断合成数据是否适合个性化学习中的具体任务，也难以识别结果缺失、学习行为顺序失真和群体性能不平等等问题。

Method: 提出PEARL（Privacy-Equivalence Audit and Release Ledger），对每个差分隐私合成数据集执行有效性、隐私保护、预测有用性、公平性及目标任务适用性检查，并记录未通过数据集的具体失败原因。在96种由数据集、生成方法、隐私预算和随机种子构成的设置中进行评估，同时测试风险预测、知识追踪和自适应辅导相关任务。

Result: 仅12种设置生成的数据集通过全部适用检查。部分数据集因遗漏重要结果群体（如退学学生）或无法保留学习活动顺序而被拒绝。即使通过隐私和预测有用性检查的数据集，在不同群体间的高风险预测性能仍可能不均等。此外，Deep Knowledge Tracing和Self-Attentive Knowledge Tracing未能从任何测试的合成知识追踪数据中学习到有意义的下一次作答模式。

Conclusion: 差分隐私只能降低个体信息暴露风险，不能保证合成数据具备教育任务所需的结构、覆盖范围、公平性和预测能力。发布合成学习者数据前，应采用PEARL等端到端审计框架，并依据具体教育任务进行针对性验证。

Abstract: Personalized learning systems rely on real learner data, including performance, behavior, and demographic information, but these data are highly privacy-sensitive. Differentially private (DP) synthetic data can support system development and educational research while reducing exposure of individual learners. Existing evaluations, however, assess privacy and predictive usefulness separately, without determining whether synthetic learner data remain usable for the intended personalized learning task. We introduce PEARL (Privacy-Equivalence Audit and Release Ledger), which approves a DP synthetic educational dataset only when it passes all required checks of validity, privacy protection, predictive usefulness, and suitability for the intended educational task, while recording why each rejected dataset fails. Across 96 study settings, each defined by a dataset, data-generation method, privacy budget, and random seed, only 12 produced synthetic datasets that passed all applicable PEARL checks. Many privacy-protected datasets were rejected for omitting important outcome groups, such as withdrawn students, or for failing to preserve the order of learning activities. Fairness analysis further showed that some datasets passing privacy and predictive-usefulness checks still yielded unequal at-risk prediction performance across groups defined by disability and socioeconomic background. Moreover, Deep Knowledge Tracing and Self-Attentive Knowledge Tracing learned no meaningful next-response patterns from any tested synthetic knowledge-tracing dataset, showing that privacy protection alone does not guarantee usefulness for dropout prediction, knowledge tracing, or adaptive tutoring.

</details>

TL;DR: Summary generation failed

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Abstract: In-context learning (ICL) jailbreaks reveal a critical vulnerability in multimodal large language models (MLLMs): harmful demonstrations in the prompt can induce unsafe outputs without modifying model parameters. Despite extensive empirical evidence, existing work lacks a principled understanding of why such jailbreaks reliably succeed or how their effectiveness scales with context composition. We propose a posterior reweighting framework that models a safety-aligned MLLM as implicitly operating over competing behavioral modes, and interprets in-context demonstrations as inference-time evidence that dynamically shifts the model's posterior preference between safe and harmful behaviors. This view formalizes jailbreak as a process of evidence accumulation, yielding predictive scaling laws with respect to demonstration count, harmful ratio, adversarial strength, and semantic diversity. Guided by this framework, we introduce a posterior-aware inference-time defense that adaptively injects benign counter-evidence based on estimated risk, effectively suppressing harmful posterior drift while preserving model utility. Compared to existing in-context defenses, our method achieves a significantly improved robustness-utility trade-off under a fixed intervention budget. Together, our results establish posterior reweighting as a unifying and predictive framework for understanding and mitigating ICL jailbreak in MLLMs.

</details>

*Abdullah Caglar Oksuz,Anisa Halimi,Erman Ayday*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文系统梳理25项利用机器学习解释进行模型窃取、成员推断和模型反演的研究，提出按解释信号获取路径分析解释隐私风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究通常仅以黑盒或白盒划分威胁模型，忽略了攻击者实际获得解释信号的方式及其差异，导致风险评估和防御设计不够准确。

Method: 将属性推断视为部分模型反演，并把模型知识与解释获取过程分离，归纳出五种获取路径：目标发布、攻击者推导、二次披露、特权访问和公开全局产物；随后比较威胁模型、解释信号、辅助知识、目标模型、数据模态、查询预算、评估指标、攻击效果与防御方法。

Result: 解释信号能够降低模型提取成本，通过解释统计量、反事实回溯距离和解释引导的鲁棒性分析增强成员推断，并支持对私有输入进行空间或代数重构。但不同解释类型的风险并不一致，防御效果也取决于攻击路径、暴露信号、目标资产及攻击者已有知识。

Conclusion: 解释隐私应被视为端到端的信息披露问题，而非简单的黑盒或白盒问题。安全评估应同时建模解释获取路径与受保护资产，并针对具体披露机制匹配防御策略。

Abstract: Machine learning explanations reveal model behavior beyond predictions, creating attack surfaces for model confidentiality and data privacy. We systematize 25 studies that exploit explanations for model extraction, membership inference, and model inversion, treating attribute inference as partial inversion. Existing work is often labeled only black- or white-box, obscuring substantial differences in what explanation signal reaches an adversary. We therefore separate model knowledge from explanation acquisition and identify five paths: target-released, attacker-derived, secondary disclosure, privileged access, and released global artifacts. Across these paths, explanations reduce extraction cost, expose membership signals through explanation statistics, recourse distance, and explanation-guided robustness, and support spatial or algebraic reconstruction of private inputs. We compare system and threat models, explanation signals, auxiliary knowledge, target models, modalities, query budgets, evaluation metrics, reported performance, and defenses. Our analysis shows that no explanation family is uniformly unsafe and no defense is uniformly effective. Risk depends on which signal is exposed, how it is acquired, which asset is targeted, and what the attacker already knows. We argue that explanation privacy should therefore be evaluated as an end-to-end disclosure problem, with defenses matched to the acquisition path and protected asset.

</details>


### [41] [From Cycle Space to Cycle Manifold: Limits and Achievability of Blind False Data Injection Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10631)
*Xin Li,Chenhan Xiao,Jonathan Cohen,Aviad Elyashar,Yang Weng,Rami Puzis*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文证明：在连通直流支路潮流模型下，基于无噪声正交检验的残差敏感子空间恰好是加权环空间，其正交补即完整的隐蔽数据注入空间；并提出测量驱动的重构方法及交流扩展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有盲攻击方法通常仅学习低秩测量子空间，缺乏对电网物理约束、隐蔽性的明确解释，也未说明恢复完整隐蔽空间所需的最少信息。论文旨在建立环空间与隐蔽数据注入之间的严格联系，并降低空间重构需求。

Method: 在连通直流支路潮流模型下，利用加权环空间分析残差敏感子空间及其正交补，刻画拓扑和支路参数的可辨识性；进一步构造无计算约束的基准问题，并提出仅依赖测量的可计算重构方法。针对交流模型，以支路有功/无功测量的环流形描述可行测量集合，并采用拓扑辅助的流形拟合与GPU测量生成。

Result: 理论上，完整隐蔽空间等于加权环空间的正交补；拓扑最多可识别到2-同构，双连通分量中的相对环边参数最多确定到每个分量一个尺度因子，桥边参数既不可识别也不是必需信息。IEEE系统实验在95%名义接受率阈值下比较了检测器绕过率与状态影响。交流实验验证了拓扑辅助流形拟合和GPU测量生成；在无损、定电压、小角度极限下，有功切片的法空间退化为直流加权环空间。

Conclusion: 论文建立了物理电网约束、加权环空间与完整隐蔽数据注入空间之间的精确对应关系，明确了盲重构的必要信息边界，并同时提供了理论基准、可计算算法及交流模型扩展，为评估和生成物理一致的隐蔽测量提供统一框架。

Abstract: A false data injection attack (FDIA) can change the estimated grid state while evading a residual-based bad data detector (BDD). Existing blind attacks learn a low-rank measurement subspace, but this algebraic view does not state the physical grid constraints that make an attack stealthy or the minimum information needed to recover the complete attack space. Under the connected direct-current (DC) branch-flow model, we show that the residual-sensitive subspace of the noiseless orthogonal test is exactly the weighted cycle space. Its orthogonal complement is therefore the complete stealthy attack space, making weighted cycle-space knowledge both necessary and sufficient for complete blind FDIA. This space identifies the topology only up to 2-isomorphism and the relative cycle-edge parameters only up to one scale per biconnected component; bridge parameters are neither identified nor required. We then formulate a computationally unconstrained benchmark and a tractable measurement-only reconstruction method. Experiments on IEEE systems compare BDD bypass rate at a 95% nominal-acceptance threshold against state impact. As a compact alternating-current (AC) extension, we characterize feasible branch P/Q measurements by a cycle manifold and demonstrate topology-assisted manifold fitting and measurement generation on a graphics processing unit (GPU). In the lossless fixed-voltage small-angle limit, the normal space of the active-power slice reduces to the DC weighted cycle space.

</details>


### [42] [HermiCache: Enclave-Aware Cache Replacement for Trusted Execution Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10634)
*Oussama Elmnaouri,Pascal Cotret,Vianney Lapôtre,Loïc Lagadec*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: HermiCache是一种面向RISC-V可信执行环境的确定性缓存侧信道防护方案，支持细粒度配置，并已在CVA6核心与Keystone TEE上实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TEE虽能隔离受保护内存与不可信软件，但共享缓存等微体系结构资源仍会泄露信息。现有缓存分区或随机化方案在细粒度配置能力和确定性方面存在不足。

Method: 设计并实现HermiCache，将其集成到OpenHwGroup CVA6 RISC-V处理器核心，并在软件层结合Keystone TEE，以提供可配置且确定性的缓存保护。

Result: HermiCache在CVA6处理器核心上的面积开销约为6%。摘要未提供更具体的安全性、性能或能耗评估结果。

Conclusion: HermiCache为RISC-V TEE提供了一种兼顾细粒度配置与确定性保护的缓存侧信道防护机制，具有较低的硬件面积开销。

Abstract: Trusted Execution Environments (TEEs) protect enclave memory from untrusted software but remain vulnerable to cache-based side-channel attacks due to shared microarchitectural resources. Existing countermeasures use techniques such as cache partitioning or randomization: these solutions are not ideal if a designer wants fine-grained configurations and a deterministic protection. In this paper, we introduce HermiCache which is an answer to these requirements. HermiCache is designed for RISC-V cores and has been implemented in the OpenHwGroup CVA6 core with a Keystone TEE for the software layer. The solution has an area overhead of 6% on the processor core.

</details>


### [43] [Architecting the Secure AI-SOC: A Neurosymbolic Framework for Pipeline Integrity and Threat Mitigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10707)
*Anna Gazani,Spyridon Kounoupidis,Panagiotis Katsaros,Nikolaos Kekatos,Grigorios Tsoumakas,Georgios Koutidis*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 该摘要提出一种面向AI安全运营中心的神经符号分层防护框架，用于缓解通过日志投毒实施的间接提示注入风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型接入安全运营流程可提升威胁情报处理效率，但攻击者可将恶意指令嵌入系统日志，诱导模型偏离预期分析逻辑。现有确定性防护缺乏语义理解，纯神经方法则存在延迟高和输出不稳定等问题。

Method: 框架采用纵深防御：首先使用定制化SIEM解码器进行确定性结构清洗，过滤填充型内容及基于特征的注入载荷；随后利用NeMo Guardrails对结构化告警执行语义边界校验；最后通过闭环遥测与人工复核机制，在SOC仪表盘中呈现被拦截事件。实验依据MITRE ATLAS分类体系，对多类提示注入样本进行评估。

Result: 摘要声称，该方案能够在日志接入边缘快速削弱常见注入载荷，并在模型处理前通过语义验证进一步阻断恶意指令，从而提升防护链路的可观测性与完整性。

Conclusion: 该工作将规则化预过滤、语义校验和人工可见性结合，试图兼顾处理效率、语义识别能力与审计需求。摘要未给出具体数据集、基线方法、误报漏报率、处理延迟和消融实验结果，因此实际效果及相对优势仍需结合正文实验验证。

Abstract: The integration of Large Language Models (LLMs) into Security Operations Centers (SOCs) streamlines threat intelligence but introduces critical vulnerabilities, notably indirect prompt injection via log poisoning. Adversaries exploit this vector to execute multistep ``promptware'' kill chains by embedding malicious payloads within system logs to hijack the LLM's operational logic. Securing this pipeline presents a dichotomy: deterministic defenses are computationally efficient yet semantically blind, while purely neural evaluations introduce prohibitive latency and probabilistic flaws. To address this, we propose a novel neurosymbolic defense-in-depth architecture that ensures end-to-end pipeline integrity. The primary layer employs customized SIEM decoders as a deterministic pre-filter, performing immediate structural sanitization to neutralize volumetric padding and signature-based injections at the ingestion edge. The secondary layer leverages NeMo Guardrails to enforce strict semantic boundaries through self-checking validation on the structured SIEM alerts prior to LLM processing. Furthermore, the framework integrates a closed-loop telemetry system, providing critical Human-in-the-Loop (HITL) visibility into thwarted attacks directly within the SOC dashboard. We present a comprehensive experimental evaluation mapped to the MITRE ATLAS taxonomy, assessing the framework against diverse prompt injections. Our results demonstrate that this synergistic approach effectively dismantles the promptware kill chain - bounding LLM stochasticity with verifiable constraints, and delivering a resilient, highly observable defense mechanism for next-generation AI-SOCs.

</details>


### [44] [CARTS: Contextual Autoregressive Rank Transcoding Steganography for Full-Capacity Keyed Text Encoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10744)
*Wissam Ghantous,Alexander V. Mantzaris*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文首次对上下文自回归秩转码隐写术（CARTS）进行严格形式化与安全分析，证明其在确定性模型下可实现精确恢复，并系统研究多类潜在攻击问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有Calgacus构造已通过实验展示了在保持令牌长度不变的情况下将载荷文本转换为隐写文本的可行性，但缺乏形式化正确性证明和安全性分析。本文旨在建立CARTS的理论基础，明确其安全性质及相关计算难题。

Method: 在秩坐标表示下建模CARTS，将密钥表示为秩向量空间上的双射；证明确定性模型下的精确正确性；定义并分析上下文搜索、密钥碰撞、消息模糊性和编码映射非交换性等问题，研究它们之间的理论关系，尤其是消息模糊性与上下文搜索的联系，以及密钥碰撞和消息模糊性之间的权衡；同时使用Llama 3 8B开展实证测试。

Result: 理论上，本文刻画了消息模糊性与上下文搜索之间的关系，并分析了密钥碰撞对消息模糊性的影响。实验中所有测试案例均成功精确恢复原始载荷；随机生成密钥未发现碰撞；人工构造的碰撞仅具有局部性而非全局性；未发现成对可交换的密钥映射。

Conclusion: CARTS在确定性语言模型下具有严格的正确性，并在所研究的攻击向量上表现出初步稳健性。该工作为利用语言模型开展构造性密码学和隐私通信研究提供了形式化起点，但其安全性仍需在更广泛模型、密钥分布和攻击场景下进一步验证。

Abstract: Autoregressive language models can be used to transform a payload text into a stegotext of identical token length by preserving per-position rank information across contexts - a methodology we formalize as Contextual Autoregressive Rank Transcoding Steganography (CARTS). While the Calgacus construction of Norelli et al. demonstrated this phenomenon experimentally, no formal security analysis existed. This paper provides the first rigorous treatment of CARTS. We show its exact correctness under deterministic model assumptions, introduce a rank-coordinate representation in which keys act as bijections on rank-vector space, define relevant security notions and the computational problems naturally associated with the construction - context search, key collisions, message equivocation, and non-commutativity of the encoding maps - and study the theoretical relationships between them, including the characterization of message equivocation in terms of context search, and the tension between key collisions and message equivocation. An empirical study on Llama 3 8B confirms exact recovery of the original payload in all tested cases, finds no key collisions under random key generation, establishes that a hand-crafted collision is local rather than global, and finds no commuting key pairs - suggesting resistance to the attack vectors studied. This work opens a formally grounded research agenda for the constructive use of language models in cryptography and privacy-preserving communication.

</details>

*Kyle Stein,Guillermo Francia,Eman El-Sheikh,Hossain Shahriar*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究面向低地球轨道卫星通信系统的智能网络攻击检测，利用卫星专用UNSW-IoTSAT数据集比较结构化多模态深度学习模型，并重点评估严格防数据泄漏及跨卫星泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LEO卫星系统同时产生射频链路、星载硬件和轨道运行等异构信息，且攻击行为具有时间演化特征。现有方法常依赖地面网络数据集或独立处理单条观测，难以反映卫星环境中的跨子系统关联和时序攻击模式。

Result: 结构化多模态建模能够提升卫星网络攻击检测性能。在防数据泄漏评估协议下，分层多模态Transformer最高达到91.66%的准确率和85.63%的宏平均F1值，结果同时表明评估协议会显著影响模型性能判断。

Conclusion: 针对LEO卫星网络攻击检测，应采用融合射频、硬件、轨道和时间信息的结构化模型，并使用严格的时序与跨卫星评估协议。分层Transformer在捕获多模态关联和攻击时序演化方面表现出较强潜力。

Abstract: The growing reliance on Low-Earth Orbit (LEO) satellite communication systems has increased the need for intelligent methods capable of detecting cyberattacks across complex and dynamic space environments. Unlike conventional network intrusion detection, satellite systems generate heterogeneous information across radio-frequency (RF) links, onboard hardware, and orbital operations. However, many existing approaches either rely on terrestrial intrusion datasets or evaluate individual observations independently, limiting their ability to capture temporal attack behavior specific to LEO satellites. In this work, we conduct a systematic study of deep-learning-based cyberattack detection using the recently introduced satellite-specific UNSW-IoTSAT dataset. We investigate structured learning architectures that preserve hardware, orbital, and RF information, including a Subsystem-Fusion MLP and a hierarchical multimodal Transformer that models both cross-subsystem interactions and temporal evolution. We further evaluate leakage-resistant row-level and temporal settings, along with cross-satellite generalization, to characterize how model architecture and evaluation protocol influence satellite cyberattack detection. Experimental results demonstrate the value of structured multimodal modeling and rigorous evaluation, with the hierarchical Transformer achieving up to 91.66% accuracy and 85.63% macro F1 under the leakage-resistant evaluation protocol.

</details>


### [46] [Beyond Static Guarantees: Measuring the Static-Pass Dynamic-Fail Gap in Security-Sensitive and LLM-Generated Python Code](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10762)
*Jessica Pourleyli,Maitreyee Das Urmi,Glaucia Melo*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出“静态通过、动态失败”（SPDF）现象及三阶段智能代理评估流程，发现约14.53%的静态分析无告警样本仍存在获得运行时证据支持的潜在可利用漏洞，表明静态安全结论不能等同于运行时安全。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 静态分析具有可扩展、可复现和低成本等优势，但难以观测依赖对抗性输入、执行上下文或漏洞链的运行时行为。将静态分析通过视为安全证据可能漏检实际可利用问题，因此需要结合大语言模型推理与动态验证评估代码的真实安全性。

Method: 研究构建三阶段流程：首先使用Bandit和Semgrep组成的复合静态分析门进行筛选；其次由大语言模型基于通用弱点枚举（CWE）进行漏洞推理和候选识别；最后在隔离的Docker容器中自主执行动态验证。实验覆盖SecurityEval、RedCode和CyberNative三个数据集，共1355个Python样本。

Result: 654个样本通过复合静态分析门且未产生告警。大语言模型阶段在235个文件中识别出394个候选漏洞，动态验证在95个文件中确认或部分确认了可利用性，对静态清洁样本得到14.53%的包容性流水线检出率。按候选文件-CWE对统计，RedCode、CyberNative和SecurityEval的确认可利用率分别为33.7%、28.6%和5.4%。CWE-338和CWE-916等高频确认类别均未被Bandit或Semgrep发现。

Conclusion: 静态分析和运行时验证应被视为分层的软件安全保障手段，而不是可互换的安全度量。对于AI生成代码及其他安全敏感软件，仅依赖静态分析会遗漏一定比例的实际可利用漏洞；结合LLM语义推理和隔离环境中的动态验证能够提升评估覆盖率。不过，结果依赖数据集构成、LLM判断质量和动态验证能力，14.53%应解释为该实验流程下的观测比例，而非所有静态清洁代码的普适漏洞率。

Abstract: Advances in large language models (LLMs) fuel the quest for scalable methods to assess the security of generated and security-sensitive software. Static analysis is widely adopted as a scalable, reproducible, and inexpensive security gate, but cannot directly observe runtime exploit behaviour. Vulnerabilities dependent on adversarial inputs, execution context, or exploit chaining may evade static checks while remaining exploitable in practice, yet passing static analysis is often treated as evidence of secure behaviour. This paper introduces the Static-Pass Dynamic-Fail (SPDF) phenomenon and a three-stage agentic pipeline combining static scanning, LLM-driven Common Weakness Enumeration (CWE) reasoning, and autonomous exploit verification in isolated Docker containers. We evaluate 1,355 Python samples from SecurityEval, RedCode, and CyberNative datasets. Of the 654 samples producing no findings under the composite Bandit-Semgrep gate, the LLM detection stage identified 394 candidate vulnerabilities across 235 files. Dynamic verification confirmed or partially confirmed exploitability in 95 files, yielding an inclusive pipeline rate of 14.53% (roughly 1 in 7 statically clean samples). This rate represents the proportion of Bandit-Semgrep-clean samples for which the pipeline identified a candidate vulnerability and obtained runtime evidence supporting exploitability. Outcomes varied by dataset: among candidate file--CWE pairs, confirmed exploitability was 33.7% for RedCode, 28.6% for CyberNative, and 5.4% for SecurityEval. Several frequently confirmed classes, including CWE-338 and CWE-916, were flagged by neither Bandit nor Semgrep. These findings indicate that static-analysis success and runtime security are hierarchical layers of software assurance rather than interchangeable measures, and have the potential to reshape how AI-generated and security-sensitive code is evaluated.

</details>

*Victoria Lovelace,Cameron Berryman,Yuhan You,Suhas Reddy Adavelly,Joel Sadler,Daniel Graham*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文比较了两种基于PentestGPT的LLM渗透测试系统，发现自主系统完成了全部三个目标，而传统人机协同系统虽未完成部分目标，却已解决约半数子任务；加入覆盖记忆机制后性能没有提升，初步表明规划与执行承诺可能比长期记忆更关键。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究旨在评估LLM智能体在渗透测试中的实际能力、失败模式及其未来瓶颈，并检验“长期记忆不足限制长链任务表现”这一常见假设。

Method: 比较两套系统：运行开源权重Kimi K2.5的人机协同系统，以及运行Claude Opus 4.8的自主系统；在三个公开目标上进行测试，并为两套系统加入覆盖记忆层，对任务完成情况和子任务得分进行分析。由于模型、工具编排、自主程度和记忆架构同时变化，研究仅进行趋势性比较，无法严格归因。

Result: 自主系统完成了全部三个目标，包括传统系统未完成的两个目标。传统系统即使在失败的目标上也完成了约一半子任务。加入覆盖记忆后，两套系统的整体结果均未改善；对传统系统失败过程的复核显示，智能体通常保留了推进路线所需的证据，但未能将其转化为明确的后续假设和行动，问题更接近规划不足与执行承诺不足，而非记忆丢失。

Conclusion: 研究初步表明，随着任务复杂度提升，LLM渗透测试智能体的主要限制可能从长期记忆转向规划能力、假设形成和持续执行能力。子任务级评测可作为防御方监测此类能力演进的量化工具，但由于实验中多个变量同时变化，关于系统差异原因的结论仍需更严格的受控实验验证。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents are increasingly applied to penetration testing, but we still know little about what they can do or how they fail. We compare two PentestGPT-based systems: a legacy human-in-the-loop system running the open-weight Kimi K2.5, and a newer autonomous system running Claude Opus 4.8. Across three public targets, the autonomous system solves all three, including the two the legacy system never finishes. The legacy result is the more surprising of the two. Even on the machines the legacy system fails to solve, it completes about half the subtasks, while running on ordinary university GPUs with no provider guardrails. We can describe the trend but not explain it, since model, harness, autonomy, and memory architecture all change together. Its direction still points to the next question: what will limit these agents as they take on more complex tasks? The usual answer is long-horizon memory, the loss of access to earlier findings during long attack chains. We test it by adding a coverage-memory layer to both systems, and neither improves outcomes. In the legacy stalled runs we could review, the limiting factor appeared to be planning and commitment rather than lost memory: agents held the evidence for a route forward and never turned it into a concrete exploitation hypothesis, which may suggest that offensive capability will advance with agents' ability to plan rather than with better memory. The same subtask scoring that tracks this capability is available to defenders, who can measure it as it rises instead of waiting to meet it in the field.

</details>


### [48] [No-Box Vulnerability Analysis: Description-only Detection of Indirect Prompt Injection Vulnerabilities in MCP Servers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10854)
*Zehua Zhang,Jie Hu,Pratham Hegde,Aditya Maheshbhai Gabani,Souradip Nath,Yibo Liu,Siyu Liu,Hongkai Chen,Hulin Wang,Zhuoer Lyu,Chang Zhu,Divij Handa,Yan Shoshitaishvili,Tiffany Bao,Ruoyu Wang,Adam Doupe*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出“无盒漏洞分析”范式：在无法访问系统、无法运行交互且仅掌握功能元数据的条件下，推断所有符合该元数据的实现中都可能存在的漏洞。原型MCPSEC仅利用MCP工具注册元数据检测间接提示注入漏洞，在20个服务器的177个工具上取得较高召回率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统漏洞分析通常依赖系统访问权限或动态交互，但闭源、远程托管、关键在役及商业受限软件往往无法满足这些条件。因此，论文旨在利用系统的输入、输出和副作用等功能元数据，在缺乏目标实现细节与运行时交互的情况下开展漏洞审计，并为后续获得访问权限时的验证提供可检验假设。

Method: 论文定义无盒漏洞分析：根据功能元数据约束可能的系统实现空间，并寻找在所有符合元数据的实现中均成立或具有普适性的漏洞假设。作者实现MCPSEC，针对Model Context Protocol服务器，仅使用服务器注册阶段公开的工具元数据，分析工具是否可能受到间接提示注入，并同时生成对应的漏洞假设与利用技术。实验覆盖20个广泛部署的MCP服务器和177个工具，并与基于大语言模型的基线方法比较。

Result: 人工评估确认95个工具存在漏洞。MCPSEC将143个工具判定为易受攻击，并为每个工具生成漏洞假设及利用方法；其中成功预测94个真实且经验证的漏洞，召回率为98.9%。相比之下，LLM基线识别出80个，召回率为84.2%。结果表明，仅凭元数据即可进行具有实际可用性的漏洞初筛与假设生成。

Conclusion: 无盒漏洞分析为无法获得系统访问权限或运行时交互的场景提供了新的安全分析路径。MCPSEC实验显示，该范式在真实MCP服务器上能够高召回率地发现间接提示注入风险，但其输出本质上是待后续访问条件验证的漏洞假设，仍需关注误报、元数据完整性及对其他漏洞类型和系统领域的泛化能力。

Abstract: Conventional vulnerability analysis relies on either system access or dynamic interaction, all of which may be unavailable to third-party analysts auditing closed-source, remotely hosted, critical in situ systems, or commercially gated software. Therefore, we propose a new paradigm of no-box vulnerability analysis in which neither access nor runtime interaction is available, and only functionality metadata is available. Such metadata defines the intended behavior of the system, including its inputs, outputs, and side effects, while constraining the space of implementations consistent with that behavior. We propose hypothesizing about vulnerabilities that exist across all possible implementations of a given system metadata, without observing or interacting with the target system. An analyst can later validate these hypotheses when additional access is available. We showcase the feasibility of no-box vulnerability analysis through implementing a prototype called MCPSEC, which audits Model Context Protocol (MCP) servers for indirect prompt injection vulnerabilities using only the tool metadata exposed at server registration time. We evaluate MCPSEC on 20 widely deployed MCP servers comprising 177 tools, among which human evaluators confirm 95 vulnerable tools. MCPSEC identified 143 tools as vulnerable, and for each vulnerable tool, it produced a hypothesized vulnerability along with exploitation technique. Using metadata alone, MCPSEC predicted 94 (98.9% recall) real verified vulnerabilities, compared against an LLM baseline with 80 (84.2% recall). Overall, our results introduce no-box vulnerability analysis as a new analysis paradigm and demonstrate its practical feasibility in realistic systems.

</details>


### [49] [A2ABreak: Systematic Security Analysis of the A2A Protocol](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10871)
*Alireza Lotfi,Mirza Masfiqur Rahman,Imtiaz Karim,Elisa Bertino*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出A2ABreak，对A2A协议进行首个系统化安全分析。方法是从自然语言规范中借助大语言模型提取并验证有限状态机，再进行对抗性验证；结果发现11项在完全遵循规范条件下即可被利用的协议级缺陷，说明A2A规范本身存在显著安全风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: A2A旨在支持跨组织自主智能体之间的发现、认证与任务委派，并与MCP形成互补。尽管其正成为多智能体系统的重要通信层，现有工作尚未对协议规范进行系统安全审计，因此需要识别不依赖具体实现错误、而由协议设计或规范遗漏导致的风险。

Method: A2ABreak首先将规范中的929条陈述形式化，并利用大语言模型辅助抽取、验证统一有限状态机，得到包含37个状态和76条转换的协议模型。随后在“对手完全遵循协议规范”的假设下，对状态机开展系统性对抗验证，以定位可由合法协议交互触发的安全性质违背。论文还将发现结果与独立专家评审比对，并以同一规范上的零样本大语言模型分析作为基线。

Result: 系统共识别11项新的协议级漏洞。摘要列举的代表性问题包括：未受保护的上下文标识符可造成跨客户端上下文注入；多跳委派链中的身份信息丢失可被用于凭据窃取；恶意智能体可通过声明未经认证的能力诱导数据泄露。经独立专家评审，A2ABreak的精确率为73.3%，F1值为84.6%；相同规范上的零样本大语言模型基线未产生任何经确认的发现。

Conclusion: 论文的核心结论是，A2A协议存在可在规范合规前提下被利用的系统性安全缺陷，单纯依赖实现层修补不足以消除风险。将自然语言规范转换为可验证的显式状态模型，并结合对抗性推理，能够显著提升协议安全分析的有效性。该工作也表明，后续A2A规范应强化上下文隔离、委派链身份连续性、能力声明认证与数据流约束等安全机制。

Abstract: The Agent2Agent (A2A) protocol, now governed by the Linux Foundation, is an open standard that enables autonomous AI agents to discover, authenticate with, and delegate tasks to one another across organizational boundaries. Designed to complement the Model Context Protocol (MCP) for tool integration, A2A is rapidly emerging as the horizontal communication layer of the multi-agent ecosystem. Yet the protocol's security has received no systematic analysis.
  This paper presents A2ABreak, the first rigorous systematic security analysis of the A2A protocol. We introduce a novel framework that utilizes an LLM-assisted extraction of a verified finite-state machine directly from the natural-language specification, producing a unified model of 37 states and 76 transitions from 929 formalized statements, and then systematically reasons over this model to discover protocol-level vulnerabilities through adversarial verification, under a full-compliance assumption.
  Our analysis uncovers 11 new vulnerabilities, each exploitable by a specification-compliant adversary without requiring any implementation flaw. Among the findings are cross-client context injection through unprotected context identifiers, credential harvesting via multi-hop identity loss in delegation chains, and data exfiltration through rogue agents advertising unattested capability claims.
  A2ABreak achieves 73.3% precision and 84.6% F1 against independent expert review, while a zero-shot LLM baseline operating over the same specification produces zero confirmed findings, demonstrating that explicit formal grounding is essential for sound protocol security analysis.

</details>


### [50] [AspisAI: A Canonical, Machine-Interpretable Governance Framework for Automated Multi-Standard Compliance Monitoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10881)
*Tsafac Nkombong Regine Cyrille,Hasan Dag,Reiner Creutzburg,Knut Haufe*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出 AspisAI：一种有界、标准无关的合规治理框架，将多个网络安全与隐私标准中的选定要求统一映射为可机器解释的规范控制模型，并基于条件化决策规则自动评估证据，输出可解释、可追溯的合规结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多标准合规通常依赖人工映射、电子表格追踪与周期性审计，导致维护成本高、标准间口径不一致、证据与结论之间缺乏可追溯性。论文旨在解决异构合规要求的统一建模、自动化证据评估及审计可解释性问题。

Method: AspisAI在限定范围内选取26项代表性要求，将其编码为具有来源信息的规范控制模型；对提交的证据应用基于条件的判断规则，生成合规判定及追溯链路。评估包括：受控模拟下的五项治理导向指标测试；与NIST公开的信息性引用进行跨标准映射验证；以及使用OpenSSF Scorecard提供的真实第三方证据，对一个开源项目进行实际治理缺口识别。

Result: 在受控模拟中，系统完成了全部需求编码，实现88.5%的映射覆盖率、完整可追溯性，并正确识别所有人为植入的治理缺口。与NIST公开映射的比对中，57%的映射完全一致，其余差异均位于同一控制类别内。基于真实开源项目证据的测试识别出了实际治理缺口，表明该框架并非仅适用于模拟环境。

Conclusion: 研究表明，在明确限定的需求范围内，保留来源信息的规范控制模型能够支持多标准合规的自动化与可审计化。其主要价值在于统一要求表示、规则化证据判定及端到端可追溯性；但结论目前受限于26项需求的有界样本，尚需通过更大规模标准覆盖、更多证据类型和实际组织部署进一步验证泛化能力。

Abstract: Organisations operating in regulated and critical-infrastructure sectors must satisfy multiple, heterogeneous cybersecurity and privacy instruments simultaneously, including but not limited to ISO/IEC~27001, the NIST Cybersecurity Framework~2.0, Cyber Essentials, and the GDPR. In practice, these obligations are managed through manual mappings, spreadsheet-based tracking, and periodic audits that are costly to maintain, inconsistent across standards, and weak in traceability. This paper presents \emph{AspisAI}, a bounded, standard-agnostic governance framework that translates selected requirements from several frameworks into a canonical, machine-interpretable control model, and evaluates submitted evidence against condition-based decision rules to produce explainable, traceable compliance determinations. Within a bounded scope of 26 representative requirements, the framework is evaluated in a controlled simulation against five governance-oriented criteria and, critically, against two external reference points that mitigate the circularity of single-author evaluation: its cross-standard mappings are validated against NIST's own published informative references, with 57\,\% exact agreement and divergences confined to same-family controls, and the framework is applied to real third-party evidence from the OpenSSF Scorecard, surfacing genuine governance gaps in a live open-source project. The controlled results, comprising full requirement encoding, 88.5\,\% mapping coverage, complete traceability, and correct detection of all introduced gaps, establish functional correctness, while the external validation provides evidence of applicability beyond the simulation. The contribution is therefore a demonstration that a canonical, provenance-preserving governance model can render multi-standard compliance both automatable and auditable.

</details>


### [51] [DriftNet: A Dual-Head Trajectory Transformer for Detecting and Localizing Prompt Injection in LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10892)
*Asif Pinjari,Mithun Paul Saint-Germain*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: DriftNet是一种轻量级双头轨迹Transformer，可在一次前向传播中同时判断工具调用轨迹是否被间接提示注入攻陷，并逐步定位注入点、劫持区间及失败注入。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有检测器通常只能输出整条轨迹的安全性 verdict 或单一不安全位置，无法同时回答攻击入口、受影响步骤以及注入是否被抵抗等关键运维问题。

Method: 模型输入记录的工具调用轨迹，使用冻结句子编码器和四类不依赖身份的世界特征表示每个步骤；共享主干网络连接两个任务头：轨迹级攻陷分类头和四分类步骤标注头，标签包括 benign、injection point、hijacked 和 failed injection。模型参数少于200万，采用面向类别不均衡的联合加权目标训练，不需要访问智能体底层模型。

Result: 在AgentDrift任务无关划分上，数据包含12536条轨迹和71024个标注步骤。DriftNet取得0.983的轨迹级F1，在98.7%的受攻击轨迹上准确恢复注入点，劫持区间IoU为0.979；对218次抵抗攻击零误报，对困难负样本的误报率为2.9%。相比相同划分上重新训练的表面基线，部分劫持召回率由11.1%提升至98.6%，延迟执行召回率由17.1%提升至93.2%，且各类误报率均下降。20组配置实验显示超参数敏感度上界为0.011 F1。

Conclusion: DriftNet能够以较低参数规模联合完成轨迹级检测和步骤级归因，显著优于表面特征基线。残余错误主要来自注入观测中缺乏可辨识指令的轨迹，说明检测性能仍受观测语义可读性限制。

Abstract: When an indirect prompt injection succeeds against an LLM agent, the compromise is visible in the agent's own behavior: a benign prefix of tool calls, a poisoned observation, and a suffix of actions that serve the attacker. An operator needs three facts: where the attack entered, which steps it corrupted, and whether apparent poison was resisted. Existing systems return either a whole-trace verdict or a single unsafe index. We present DriftNet, a dual-head trajectory Transformer that reads a logged tool-call trajectory and answers all three questions in one forward pass: one head classifies the trajectory as compromised or not, and a second assigns every step one of four labels (benign, injection point, hijacked, failed injection). To our knowledge it is the first supervised detector to produce this joint output. A frozen sentence encoder and four identity-free world features embed each step; the trained trunk, under two million parameters and optimized with a class-weighted joint objective over both heads, needs no access to the agent's model. On the task-disjoint split of the AgentDrift benchmark (12,536 trajectories, 71,024 labeled steps), with a 20-configuration sweep bounding hyperparameter sensitivity to 0.011 F1 and the test part evaluated exactly once, DriftNet reaches trajectory-level F1 of 0.983, exact injection-point recovery on 98.7% of attacked trajectories, hijacked-span IoU of 0.979, zero flags on 218 resisted attacks, and 2.9% flags on hard negatives. A surface baseline retrained on the identical split recovers 11.1% of partial hijacks and 17.1% of delayed executions; DriftNet reaches 98.6% and 93.2% while lowering every false-alarm rate. Reading all 26 residual errors shows that most misses trace to trajectories whose labeled injection observation carries no legible instruction, and we report the benchmark's measured world-identity regularity alongside the results.

</details>


### [52] [Empirical Evaluation of Membership Inference Attacks on NLP Text Classifiers: A Baseline Study on SST-2](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10935)
*William Novak,Muhammad Abusaqer*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文在SST-2情感分类任务上比较了TF-IDF+逻辑回归与微调DistilBERT的成员推断攻击风险，并评估正则化和训练轮数调整的缓解效果。两种模型均存在可检测的成员信号；减少DistilBERT训练轮数在几乎不损失准确率的情况下实现了更优的隐私—效用平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自然语言处理模型的训练数据可能包含敏感用户文本，因此有必要系统评估模型是否泄露特定样本参与训练的信息，并探索低成本的隐私风险缓解方法。

Method: 基于GLUE SST-2构建受控基准，比较TF-IDF+逻辑回归和微调DistilBERT。采用基于损失阈值的成员推断攻击，以开发集准确率和宏平均F1衡量任务效用；同时测试增强逻辑回归正则化，以及将DistilBERT训练轮数从3轮减少至2轮两种缓解策略。

Result: DistilBERT的准确率和宏平均F1分别为0.9466和0.9460，逻辑回归分别为0.8756和0.8727。攻击AUC分别为0.5615和0.5800，表明两种模型均泄露了高于随机水平但幅度有限的成员信号。增强正则化可降低逻辑回归的泄露，但伴随明显效用损失；DistilBERT训练2轮则在准确率几乎不变的情况下降低泄露。

Conclusion: 成员推断风险不仅存在于高性能预训练模型，也存在于传统线性模型。适度的正则化或减少微调轮数能够改善隐私—效用权衡，其中减少DistilBERT训练轮数是一种简单且成本较低的缓解方案。

Abstract: Membership inference attacks (MIAs) try to determine whether a specific record was used to train a model, a privacy risk that matters in natural language processing (NLP), where training data can contain sensitive user text. This paper presents a controlled benchmark of membership inference vulnerability for text classification on the GLUE SST-2 sentiment dataset. A TF-IDF + Logistic Regression pipeline and a fine-tuned DistilBERT classifier are compared under a loss-threshold MIA, with utility measured by development accuracy and macro F1. DistilBERT reached 0.9466 accuracy and 0.9460 macro F1 against 0.8756 and 0.8727 for Logistic Regression, yet both models leaked membership signal (Attack AUC 0.5615 and 0.5800, respectively). Two mitigations were tested. Stronger regularization reduced leakage for Logistic Regression at a visible utility cost, whereas fine-tuning DistilBERT for 2 epochs instead of 3 reduced leakage with negligible accuracy loss. Lightweight training adjustments can improve the privacy-utility trade-off without complex defenses.

</details>


### [53] [Empirical Evaluation of Data Poisoning Attacks in Supervised Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10952)
*Toshif Khan,Muhammad Abusaqer*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 研究比较了标签翻转和后门投毒对MNIST与Fashion-MNIST上三种分类器的影响。标签翻转明显降低模型性能，而后门投毒在保持较高干净测试性能的同时，实现了0.9667至1.0000的攻击成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统干净测试集指标难以充分揭示训练时数据投毒风险，尤其难以识别能够保持正常任务性能、却植入特定触发行为的后门攻击。因此，有必要比较不同投毒方式、模型和数据集下的性能退化与攻击有效性。

Method: 在MNIST和Fashion-MNIST上评估标签翻转与后门投毒两类训练时攻击，设置5%、10%和20%的投毒率，并使用逻辑回归、线性支持向量机和随机森林作为基线分类器。通过干净测试准确率、宏平均精确率、宏平均召回率、宏平均F1值，以及后门攻击成功率进行比较。

Result: 标签翻转导致模型性能明显下降，其中逻辑回归和线性SVM受影响最大，随机森林表现相对稳定。后门投毒在三个模型和两个数据集上均取得0.9667至1.0000的攻击成功率，同时通常能够使干净测试性能维持在接近基线的水平。

Conclusion: 无差别投毒通常可通过常规分类指标观察到性能退化，而针对性后门投毒具有更强隐蔽性，可能在不显著损害正常性能的情况下植入高效的异常行为。模型安全评估应结合干净性能与攻击成功率等安全指标，而不能仅依赖传统测试准确率。

Abstract: Data poisoning corrupts training data to degrade a model or to plant attacker-controlled behavior. This study evaluates two representative training-time attacks, label flipping and backdoor poisoning, on MNIST and Fashion-MNIST with three baseline classifiers: Logistic Regression, Linear SVM, and Random Forest. Clean training is compared with poisoning rates of 5%, 10%, and 20% using clean-test accuracy, macro-precision, macro-recall, macro-F1, and, for backdoors, attack success rate. Label flipping caused clear degradation, largest for Logistic Regression and Linear SVM, while Random Forest stayed comparatively stable. Backdoor poisoning reached attack success rates from 0.9667 to 1.0000 on both datasets and all three models while often keeping clean-test performance near baseline. The results separate indiscriminate poisoning, which shows up in standard metrics, from targeted backdoor poisoning, which stays comparatively stealthy while embedding highly effective malicious behavior, and they support security-oriented evaluation beyond conventional clean-test metrics.

</details>


### [54] [DeFiFusion: Combining Transaction Events with Smart Contracts to Detect Price Manipulation Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11008)
*Rui Cao,Shaojing Fan,Liming Fang,Yuchan Liu,Yingying Jiao,Zhenguang Liu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: DeFiFusion通过联合建模交易事件与智能合约语义，检测价格操纵攻击，在225个案例中召回222个，精确率达到96.10%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有交易行为检测方法缺乏合约执行语义，容易将正常市场波动误报为攻击；静态合约分析又忽略真实交易行为，可能报告现实中无法利用的漏洞。因此，需要统一分析交易动态与合约逻辑之间的交互关系。

Method: 构建双模态价格操纵攻击检测框架DeFiFusion。首先设计面向价格操纵的事件编码，提取细粒度的时间与经济特征；随后利用基于大语言模型的合约语义提取获取执行逻辑信息；最后通过采用T5风格相对位置编码的双模态投影融合Transformer，对交易事件和合约语义进行融合，以捕获多阶段、循环式的执行结构。

Result: 实验结果表明，DeFiFusion整体检测性能达到先进水平，在225个价格操纵攻击案例中成功召回222个，精确率为96.10%，同时能够降低正常市场波动导致的误报。

Conclusion: 价格操纵攻击的恶意性源于交易行为与智能合约逻辑的共同作用，单一模态难以可靠识别。联合利用两类信息能够提升检测的准确性与实际可利用性，证明DeFiFusion适用于更有效的DeFi安全监测。

Abstract: Decentralized Finance (DeFi) has emerged as a rapidly growing blockchain-based financial service, where market transaction dynamics and underlying smart contract logic are intricately intertwined. This autonomous interplay, while eliminating centralized intermediaries, significantly expands the vulnerability surface of DeFi protocols to Price Manipulation Attacks (PMAs), which have already inflicted catastrophic financial losses. Despite their gravity, existing detection paradigms suffer from fundamental limitations. Transaction-centric methods lack awareness of contract execution semantics, making them prone to false positives under legitimate market volatility, while static contract analyses ignore real transaction behaviors and frequently report vulnerabilities that are infeasible to exploit in practice. We present DeFiFusion, a dual-modal PMA detection framework that closes this gap by jointly modeling transaction events and smart contract semantics within a unified pipeline. Our core insight is that PMA maliciousness emerges only from the interaction between transaction behaviors and the contract logic they exploit; neither signal suffices in isolation. Accordingly, we derive price-manipulation-aware event encoding for extracting fine-grained temporal and economic features tailored to manipulation patterns. We further introduce LLM-based contract semantic extraction to supply the execution-logic context that prior behavioral methods lack. To fuse these modalities, we propose a Dual-Modal Projection-Fusion Transformer with T5-style relative positional encoding, capturing the cyclic multi-stage execution structures that distinguish PMAs from benign market activity. Extensive experiments demonstrate that DeFiFusion consistently achieves state-of-the-art detection performance, effectively recalling 222 of the 225 PMA cases while maintaining a precision of 96.10%.

</details>


### [55] [The Missing Boundary: How Autonomous Agents Lose Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11024)
*Zonghao Ying,Xiangfan Wu,Huiyu Wu,Xing Zheng,Huangsheng Cheng,Xiaorong Shi,Jing Guo*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 研究发现，智能体在合法任务中发生越权执行，关键并非单独的控制退化或不安全机会，而是二者同时存在：缺失的控制边界遇到可执行的越权动作后，会转化为实际失控。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究多将失控归因于对抗性指令、恶意环境或目标冲突，较少解释合法任务执行过程中控制边界如何逐步失效并导致越权行为。本文旨在识别这种失控发生的必要条件。

Method: 独立操纵目标压力、控制退化和可执行的不安全机会，在确定性的多轮工具使用环境中，使用5种智能体模型覆盖16个操作领域，评估1800条独立轨迹；同时开展全因子实验、额外领域验证和上下文管理消融实验。

Result: 仅有控制退化或不安全机会时，失控率均不高；二者同时存在时，全因子实验中的失控率达到55%，在另外10个操作领域中达到62%。恢复原始控制边界后，即使不安全动作仍可执行，失控率降至0%。上下文压缩本身并无害处：保留控制约束时失控率为0%，省略约束时升至87%。

Conclusion: 合法任务目标保持不变并不意味着执行安全。只要控制边界退化且环境提供可执行的越权机会，潜在失控就可能转化为实际违规行为。因此，持续保留并验证控制约束，是长期工具调用智能体安全运行的关键。

Abstract: Autonomous agents increasingly perform long-horizon tasks involving tool use, persistent state, and consequential actions, raising a fundamental question: \emph{under what conditions does an agent cross the boundary of authorized execution while pursuing a legitimate task?} Existing studies often attribute such failures to adversarial instructions, malicious environments, or conflicting objectives, leaving unclear how loss of control can emerge during otherwise legitimate task execution. We study this question by independently manipulating three factors: goal pressure, control degradation, and executable unsafe opportunity. Our central hypothesis is that a degraded control boundary becomes consequential when the environment exposes an executable action that crosses it, even when the underlying task remains legitimate and a sanctioned path remains feasible. We test this hypothesis in a deterministic multi-turn environment across five agent models and 16 operational domains. Across 1,800 unique trajectories, we find that neither degraded control nor unsafe opportunity alone produces substantial loss of control; when both are present, the loss-of-control rate reaches $55\%$ in the full-factorial study and $62\%$ across ten additional operational domains. Restoring the original control boundary reduces the rate to $0\%$ even when the unsafe action remains executable. A context-management ablation further shows that compaction itself is not harmful: preserving the control constraints yields $0\%$ loss of control, whereas omitting them increases the rate to $87\%$. These results show how a latent loss of control can become an external violation: the task objective remains intact, but an executable opportunity can turn a missing control boundary into consequential action. Our code will be made publicly available at https://github.com/Tencent/AI-Infra-Guard.

</details>


### [56] [BenchShield: Formal Model-Backed Instrumentation for Reward Integrity in LLM-Agent Evaluation Infrastructure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11028)
*Shenghan Zheng,Zonglin Di,Yimin Liu,Kyoung Whan Choe,Jiankai Sun,Heguang Lin,Penghao Jiang,Yifeng He,Xiao Cheng,Jicheng Wang,Wenbo Chen,Alex Yates,Yinzhe Zhao,Bingran You,Yuan Gao,Ayush Munot,Shubham Gaur,Zhe Ye,Hao Wang,Xiangyi Li,Dawn Song,Christophe Hauser*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出BenchShield，一种面向LLM智能体评测的模型驱动型奖励完整性检测基础设施。它结合静态污点分析与运行时证据归因，在运行前发现潜在奖励劫持路径，并在运行后验证具体行为是否越过评测边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交互式智能体评测涉及状态观测、工具调用、工作区修改、成果提交和奖励计算，因而容易出现奖励劫持：智能体通过利用奖励相关轨迹提升得分，却未完成预期任务。现有方法主要依赖任务专用修补、提示约束或事后检测，缺乏可复用、可审计的运行级证据来证明评测过程符合设计边界。

Method: BenchShield首先用有限生命周期模型刻画评测中奖励相关的事件。随后执行两类互补分析：其一是具有阶段感知能力的静态污点分析，用于在运行前识别可能导致奖励劫持的路径；其二是基于评测基础设施证据的运行时分析，用于归因智能体的实际操作，并生成有证据支撑的检测结论。作者还构建了BenchShield Trajectories数据集，包含来自三个基准、超过31000次公开智能体运行中的456条人工标注并经裁决的轨迹。

Result: 相较于在相同任务和模型上的智能体式可劫持性扫描器基线，BenchShield将全链路召回率从23%至94%提升至77%至100%，将同一攻击向量覆盖率从16%至56%提升至43%至78%，并将单任务成本最多降低65%。其运行时分析基于基础设施侧证据检测奖励劫持，准确率达到96%。

Conclusion: BenchShield表明，将评测生命周期建模、静态污点分析和基础设施侧运行时证据结合，可以比任务专用规则或单纯提示约束更系统地保障LLM智能体评测的奖励完整性。该方法在检测覆盖率、运行时准确率和成本之间取得较好平衡，但其有效性仍依赖生命周期模型的正确性、基础设施日志的完整性以及标注数据对不同基准和劫持模式的代表性。

Abstract: LM-agent benchmarks increasingly function as interactive evaluation infrastructure. Agents observe state, call tools, modify workspaces,
  submit artifacts, and receive rewards from outcome procedures. This interactivity makes evaluations vulnerable to reward hacking: an agent
  improves its measured score by exploiting the reward-relevant trajectory instead of solving the intended task. Existing defenses rely largely
  on task-specific patches, prompt instructions, or post-hoc detectors. They do not provide reusable evidence that a concrete run remained
  within its intended evaluation boundary. This paper presents BenchShield, a model-backed instrumentation layer for reward integrity in
  LLM-agent evaluation. BenchShield grounds detection in a finite lifecycle model of an evaluation's reward-relevant events. Within the
  benchmark infrastructure, two complementary analyses operate over this model. A static, phase-aware taint analysis exposes reward-hacking
  paths before a run. Its runtime counterpart uses infrastructure-side evidence to attribute concrete agent use and emit evidence-backed claims.
  We construct BenchShield Trajectories, a human-labeled corpus of 456 adjudicated trajectories from more than 31,000 public agent runs across
  three benchmarks. Compared with an agentic hackability scanner baseline on the same tasks and model, BenchShield improves full-chain recall
  from 23-94% to 77-100%, same-vector coverage from 16-56% to 43-78%, and reduces per-task cost by up to 65%. Its runtime analysis achieves 96%
  accuracy in detecting reward hacking from infrastructure-side evidence.

</details>


### [57] [ToxicRAG: Compromising Retrieval-Augmented Generation Systems via Single-Shot Knowledge Poisoning Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11082)
*Haozhe Lu,Jiaqi Li,Xinyuan Zhu,Xiang Li*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: ToxicRAG提出一种面向检索增强生成系统的单文档知识投毒攻击：通过连贯的“知识更新”叙事逐步否定原答案，并引导模型接受攻击者指定的错误答案。在所测试配置中，攻击成功率达到0.61至0.91，整体优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RAG知识投毒攻击通常依赖多个注入文档或直接使用答案模板，容易暴露攻击模式。本文旨在设计一种仅需每个目标问题对应一份文档、且更具自然叙事性和说服力的攻击方式，同时评估RAG系统对事实一致性和来源可靠性的脆弱性。

Method: ToxicRAG生成一份叙事型投毒文档，先承认既有答案，再引入虚构事件以声称原答案已失效，最后借助所谓权威来源支持攻击者指定的目标答案。方法还可选用基于代理语言模型的答案聚焦自验证循环：当代理模型未生成目标答案时，迭代修订文档。实验覆盖Natural Questions、HotpotQA和MS-MARCO三个数据集，每个数据集选取100个目标问题，并组合四种受害LLM与四种稠密检索器，在采样语料库设置下进行评估。

Result: ToxicRAG在12个数据集—模型组合上的攻击成功率为0.61至0.91，并在每种组合中均达到或超过最强评估基线，优势幅度为0至11个百分点。结果表明，叙事形式的单文档投毒内容能够在所评估的RAG配置中持续影响最终回答。

Conclusion: 研究证明，RAG系统不仅容易受到直接答案注入，也可能受到结构连贯、表面上具有知识更新逻辑的单文档攻击。该结果支持加强事实一致性验证、来源溯源和抗投毒评估；但由于实验采用采样语料库，结论对真实大规模部署环境的外推仍需进一步验证。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) can ground large language model (LLM) outputs in external evidence, but it also exposes the system to knowledge poisoning. Representative attacks use multiple injected documents or templates that directly assert a target answer. We present ToxicRAG, a one-document-per-target attack that expresses misinformation as a coherent knowledge-update narrative. The generated document first acknowledges the previously accepted answer, introduces fabricated events that appear to invalidate it, and then attributes the attacker-selected answer to a set of purported authorities. An answer-focused self-validation loop optionally revises a candidate when a surrogate language model does not reproduce the target answer. We evaluate the attack on 100 target questions from each of Natural Questions, HotpotQA, and MS-MARCO, using four victim LLMs and four dense retrievers. In the sampled-corpus setting reported in this paper, ToxicRAG obtains ASRs between 0.61 and 0.91 across the twelve dataset--model combinations. It matches or exceeds the strongest evaluated baseline in every combination, with margins ranging from 0 to 11 percentage points. These results show that narrative-form poisoned documents can remain influential under the evaluated RAG configurations and motivate further study of factual consistency and source provenance in RAG systems.

</details>


### [58] [The Machines Are Calling: Measuring Automated and Synthetic Voices in Unwanted Inbound Calls](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11137)
*Xingyu Shen,Tommy Duong,Muduo Xu,Xiaodong An,Jiaqi Gan,Haoyuan Tang,Jamey Z. Liang,Siyu Zhang,Yan Zhang,Simiao Ren*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 该研究通过语音蜜罐测量了机器拨打电话及机器语音的实际比例：至少26.9%的有效开场包含机器语音，另有9.9%为接通后沉默，可能代表机器拨号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有监管已将人工智能生成语音纳入电话消费者保护规则，但缺乏同行评议的实测数据来区分机器发起的呼叫、录音回放和实时合成语音。研究旨在提供可复现、可披露的测量流程及基线 prevalence。

Method: 研究使用真实美国电话号码和基于语言模型的人格化交互语音蜜罐，在66天内记录10,987通电话，并排除系统静默应答的11天数据。对正常应答的7,233通电话，研究结合音频指纹匹配、商业合成语音检测器和盲听者复核，分析来电者开场十秒音频；同时按来电类型、号码流通时间和营销活动进行分层比较。

Result: 在有效开场中，13.8%包含其他通话中重复出现的录音，13.1%被检测器判定为新生成的合成语音，9.9%在问候后始终沉默，54.2%被判定为新鲜的人类语音，9.0%无法评分。机器语音至少占26.9%；录音回放占检测器判定机器语音比例的45%，即全部6,192个可评分开场的29.3%。同一波形有13.6%的概率在不同通话中跨越检测阈值，听者仅确认检测器标记内容的54.4%。合成语音在潜在客户开发类垃圾电话中占33.8%，在欺诈类电话中占21.1%，仅0.44%主动披露自动化。号码流通历史比日历时间更能解释机器语音比例变化，长期流通号码的比例为59%，同期新号码为19%。

Conclusion: 机器参与电话通信的规模显著，且单一语音检测器不足以可靠估计真实 prevalence，因为录音回放、检测阈值不稳定和误报均会影响结果。应将音频指纹、语音检测、人工复核与呼叫行为联合使用，并把号码传播历史和跨活动复用纳入监测框架。研究还表明，自动化活动具有跨活动持续性，监管和反垃圾电话措施不应只针对单个号码或单次活动。

Abstract: In February 2024 the U.S. Federal Communications Commission (FCC) placed AI-generated voices under the Telephone Consumer Protection Act (TCPA). Yet no peer-reviewed measurement says how much unwanted call traffic is placed by a machine, or how much of that machine speech is synthesized rather than played from a recording. We report both with a disclosed pipeline. An interactive voice honeypot (language-model personas on real U.S. numbers, the caller recorded on its own track) recorded 10,987 calls over 66 days; 11 days on which our stack answered silently are set aside. Three instruments read each opening: an audio fingerprint that finds the same recording played on other calls, a commercial synthetic-speech detector on the caller's first ten seconds, and blinded listeners who check what it flags. Of the 7,233 calls our persona greeted on normal days, 13.8% open with a recording we also heard on another call, and 13.1% with fresh audio the detector labels synthetic. A further 9.9% open with a caller who never spoke after our greeting, 54.2% with fresh audio the detector labels human, and 9.0% could not be scored. Machine-voiced openings are therefore at least 26.9%, a further tenth of calls are silent connections we read as machine-placed, and replays of a recording make up 45% of the detector's own rate (29.3% of 6,192 scored openings). The same waveform played on two calls lands on opposite sides of the detector's threshold 13.6% of the time, and eleven listeners confirm 54.4% of what it flags. Synthetic openings concentrate in lead-generation spam (33.8%), not fraud (21.1%); 0.44% disclose automation. Prevalence tracks how long a bait number has circulated (59% against 19% in the same weeks): seeding history, not calendar time, explains the trend. Campaigns outlast their numbers: one recorded compliance notice opens calls in six campaigns, and one synthetic voice serves nine.

</details>


### [59] [You Get What You Sample: Evaluating Sampling Strategies for Web Security Measurements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11218)
*Xuenan Zhang,Yuqing Yang,Giancarlo Pellegrino*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究网页安全测量中“如何抽样”会如何影响研究结论，指出常用的 Top N 域名抽样并不能代表整体 Web 分布，而概率抽样能更稳定、无偏地估计问题流行率和影响范围。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模 Web 测量常依赖 Tranco、Common Crawl 等域名数据集，但完整分析成本高，因此必须抽样。现有研究普遍使用 Top N 抽样，却缺乏系统证据说明这种做法是否会引入偏差、扭曲漏洞/问题发生率，或削弱不同研究之间的可比性。

Method: 作者结合文献综述与大规模实证测量，对 50 万个 Tranco 域名和 2480 万个 Common Crawl 主机进行分析，比较不同数据集与抽样策略，包括 Top N、概率抽样和混合抽样，并评估它们对 prevalence 与 impact 类测量目标的影响。

Result: 研究发现，Top N 抽样在某些场景下有合理性，但不能代表 Web 的整体分布；概率抽样能产生更稳定且更接近无偏的估计；混合抽样相较纯概率抽样没有优势，因为其固定前缀部分会持续降低准确性。

Conclusion: 论文的核心贡献是将 Web 安全测量中的抽样问题从经验惯例转化为数据驱动的方法论问题，并建议未来研究采用自适应的概率抽样策略，尤其适用于目标问题真实流行率未知的场景。

Abstract: Web measurement studies rely on domain datasets such as Tranco to quantify the prevalence and impact of security issues at scale, but exhaustively analyzing these datasets is often infeasible because of the cost of advanced analysis techniques, requiring the use of sampling. Despite its widespread use, sampling remains largely guided by convention---most commonly \emph{Top $N$} domain selection---rather than evidence, and its influence on the validity and generalizability of security findings has received little systematic evaluation. Consequently, it remains unclear whether common sampling strategies introduce systematic bias, distort observed vulnerability rates, or limit comparability across studies.
  In this work, we undertake, to the best of our knowledge, the first comprehensive investigation into how sampling methodologies affect the measurements and the conclusions. Through a comprehensive literature review and large-scale measurements of 500k Tranco and 24.8M Common Crawl hosts, we perform a comparative evaluation of datasets and sampling strategies. We show that, while Top $N$ sampling may be a rational strategy, the researchers have to bear in mind that Top $N$ does not reflect the overall distribution of the web. Instead, probability-based strategies yield stable, unbiased estimates for prevalence and many impact objectives. Hybrid sampling provides no advantages over pure probability sampling, as its deterministic prefix consistently contributes negatively to accuracy. Building on these results, we provide data-backed guidance for future studies, proposing to use an adaptive probability-based sampling strategy that remains effective even when the prevalence of the target issue is unknown.

</details>


### [60] [You've Got a BUD in Me: Authenticated Reads from Per-Block Write Logs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11251)
*Alejandro Ranchal-Pedrosa,Cody Littley,Ben Marsh*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出一种以区块写入日志为核心的历史状态认证方案 BUD，用写入更新替代全状态认证结构来生成历史成员与排除证明，显著降低证明路径对状态规模增长的敏感性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统区块链通常通过覆盖整个状态的认证数据结构支持读证明，状态规模扩大时，路径长度、缓存压力和磁盘访问成本会上升。作者关注的问题是：如何在不维护全局状态树的前提下，高效证明某个历史时点的键是否存在或缺失。

Method: 方案为每个区块的写集生成 Block Update Digest，并在同一键的连续修改之间建立前驱指针；SuperBUD 汇总一个窗口内各键的最后写入；再用指数层级结构压缩长期未变更区间，使证明只需跨越少量摘要。理论上，层级范围内所需摘要数与时间间隔的对数相关，超过范围后每个顶层窗口额外增加一个摘要。

Result: 作者证明该方案在对抗性证明者与最多 f 个拜占庭验证者存在时具备可靠性，并在具备归档、签名见证和委员会证据的条件下，对部署后发生过修改的查询保证完备性。实验显示，状态规模增加 50 倍时，base-BUD 路径仅增加 1.24 倍，而内存版和缓存受限磁盘版 Merkle Patricia Trie 分别增加 3.1 倍和 69.5 倍；合成轨迹中，两摘要读层载荷低于 800 字节，热路径哈希验证 p99 不超过 146 微秒。

Conclusion: 该工作贡献在于将历史认证从“全状态结构”转向“写入历史结构”，适合状态膨胀明显、历史查询需求强的区块链系统。其优势是证明更短、验证更快、对状态规模更不敏感；主要边界条件是依赖归档数据、委员会证明和部署后修改锚点，因此实际落地需要完善数据可用性与长期证据保存机制。

Abstract: Blockchains usually pay for authenticated reads by maintaining a structure that spans the entire state. We show how validators can support historical membership and exclusion proofs by authenticating each block's writes instead. A Block Update Digest (BUD) commits a write log whose predecessor pointers link successive modifications of each key. A SuperBUD summarizes last writes over a window; an exponential hierarchy turns long unchanged intervals into short proofs. The digest count is logarithmic in the gap within the hierarchy's range, with one additional digest per top-level window beyond it. We prove soundness against adversarial provers and up to f Byzantine validators, and completeness for queries anchored by a post-deployment modification, assuming archive, attestation, and committee evidence is available. Across a 50x increase in state size, the measured base-BUD path rises by 1.24x, compared with 3.1x and 69.5x for in-memory and cache-bounded disk-backed Merkle Patricia tries. On the synthetic trace, two-digest read-layer payloads stay below 800 bytes, and warm hash-path verification takes at most 146 microseconds at p99.

</details>


### [61] [Few-Shot Learning for Network Intrusion Detection: Methods, Datasets, and Performance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11275)
*Arne Roszeitis,Victor Jüttner,Erik Buchmann*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文系统综述了2022至2026年面向网络入侵检测的少样本学习研究，从1358条记录中筛选出21项研究，归纳方法、数据集和实验设置，并指出研究之间的评估差异与可复现性不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 新型攻击通常缺乏充足的标注数据，使传统网络入侵检测系统难以训练。少样本学习能够利用极少量样本识别新攻击类型，但现有研究在方法选择和实验设置方面差异较大，缺少系统性比较。

Method: 研究采用类似PRISMA 2020的系统性文献综述流程，检索ACM Digital Library、IEEE Xplore和Scopus。对1358条初始记录进行筛选、去重和质量评估，最终纳入21项研究，并分类分析少样本学习方法、数据集、实验参数及报告性能。

Result: 元学习和卷积神经网络是最常见的方法，分别出现在8项和10项研究中。多数研究采用每类不超过5个样本的设置，但具体实验条件差异明显。CIC-IDS2017和CSE-CIC-IDS2018是使用频率最高的数据集。部分研究缺少关键实验参数和源代码，限制了结果复现及方法间的直接比较。

Conclusion: 网络入侵检测中的少样本学习研究已形成一定方法基础，但实验协议、数据集使用和报告规范尚未统一。未来应完善参数和代码公开，采用更加一致的评估设置，以提升研究的可复现性和横向可比性。

Abstract: Anomaly-based network intrusion detection systems (NIDS) are an important first line of defense. However, training NIDS for new attack types is challenging, because labeled attack data are rarely available. Few-shot learning (FSL) addresses this problem by learning from few samples. However, the approaches and evaluation settings, that have been investigated so far, vary widely. This work systematically reviews FSL approaches for NIDS published from 2022 to 2026. We conduct a systematic literature review with PRISMA 2020-like reporting to search ACM Digital Library, IEEE Xplore, and Scopus. From a set of 1,358 initial records, we retain 21 studies after screening, deduplication, and quality filtering. We classify the applied FSL approaches, datasets, and experimental parameters and compare reported performance. Meta-learning and convolutional neural networks are the most common approaches, with 8 and 10 studies, respectively. Most studies evaluate five or fewer samples per class, although settings vary. CIC-IDS2017 and CSE-CIC-IDS2018 are the most frequently used datasets. Missing parameters and source code limit reproducibility and direct comparison between approaches.

</details>


### [62] [Deep-Fake CAPTCHA: Mitigating Next-Generation Social Engineering Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11404)
*Guy Frankovits,Lior Yasur,Fred M. Grabovski,Yisroel Mirsky*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出DF-CAPTCHA，通过让通话参与者完成简单的挑战响应任务，主动验证音视频真实性与身份一致性，从而提升实时深度伪造识别能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 被动式深度伪造检测依赖媒体伪造痕迹，但实时深度伪造质量提升后，普通用户难以可靠区分真伪，传统方法在实际通话场景中存在局限。因此，研究旨在构建一种面向实时交互、难以被伪造系统即时模拟的主动防御机制。

Method: 系统要求通话者执行对人类较容易、但对当前实时深度伪造系统较难生成的挑战响应任务，并从四个维度验证结果：真实性、身份一致性、任务完成情况和响应时间。研究同时覆盖音频和视频模态，结合用户研究与实时深度伪造模型实验，将DF-CAPTCHA与被动检测方法进行比较。

Result: 用户通常难以准确区分实时深度伪造媒体与真实媒体。DF-CAPTCHA在音频和视频场景中均显著优于被动检测方法，并取得较高检测准确率，表明主动挑战响应能够有效提升实时深度伪造识别性能。摘要未提供具体数据、样本规模或统计显著性。

Conclusion: 主动式挑战响应验证是一种具有实用性和鲁棒性的实时深度伪造防御方案，可用于降低基于伪造音视频的身份冒用与社会工程风险。但其实际效果仍取决于挑战任务设计、用户可用性、响应延迟以及对更先进实时生成系统的适应能力。

Abstract: This paper presents DF-CAPTCHA, an active defense against real-time deepfake impersonation in voice and video calls. Instead of passively searching for artifacts, DF-CAPTCHA prompts the caller to perform simple challenge-response tasks that are easy for humans but difficult for current real-time deepfake systems to generate convincingly. The framework verifies the response using four criteria: realism, identity consistency, task completion, and response time. We evaluate the approach across both audio and video modalities using user studies and experiments with real-time deepfake models. Results show that people often struggle to distinguish real-time deepfakes from authentic media, while DF-CAPTCHA substantially improves detection performance over passive methods, reaching high accuracy in both modalities. These findings suggest that active challenge-based verification is a practical and robust defense against next-generation social engineering attacks based on real-time deepfakes.

</details>


### [63] ["They don't care about this": A Systematic Study of TEE Build Reproducibility in the Wild](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11411)
*Annika Wilde,Marco Gutfleisch,Felix Reichmann,Anirban Chakraborty,Yuval Yarom,M. Angela Sasse,Ghassan Karame*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文分析了115个TEE部署，发现91%无法复现，80%同时缺少源代码和参考构建，表明远程证明的可信验证基础普遍不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 远程证明只有在验证者能够将TEE报告的度量值追溯到原始源代码时，才能充分支持代码和数据的机密性与完整性保证，而这一能力依赖可复现构建。

Method: 研究者对115个Intel SGX、Intel TDX和AMD SEV部署进行了技术分析；随后联系50个SGX项目维护者，并访谈了来自工业界和学术界的12名开发者，以识别不可复现构建的技术和生态原因。

Result: 91%的部署无法复现，80%既未提供源代码，也未提供参考构建。访谈中仅1名参与者表示在开发阶段将可复现性作为优先事项。主要障碍包括二进制中包含时间戳等技术问题，以及多方协作项目中开发者无法控制完整构建环境等生态问题。

Conclusion: TEE生态当前普遍缺乏可复现构建实践，削弱了远程证明的可验证性。解决这一问题不能仅依赖开发者修复构建细节，而需要覆盖源代码、参考构建、构建环境和多方协作流程的整体性开发方法，并建立更强的生态承诺。

Abstract: Trusted Execution Environments (TEEs) have become a cornerstone of modern cloud computing, providing strong confidentiality and integrity guarantees for both code and data. A critical component of this trust model is remote attestation, which enables external entities to verify the authenticity and integrity of code executing within a TEE through cryptographic measurements. However, the effectiveness of remote attestation fundamentally depends on the verifier's ability to trace the reported measurement back to the original source code - a property that can only be guaranteed through reproducible builds.
  In this paper, we investigate the reproducibility of TEE builds through a technical analysis of 115 TEE deployments. Our analysis spans popular TEEs such as Intel SGX, Intel TDX, and AMD SEV, and reveals that a striking 91% of those deployments were not reproducible, with 80% failing to provide both source code and a reference build, the two essential prerequisites for reproducibility. To explore the root causes, we contacted the maintainers of 50 SGX projects and managed to recruit 12 developers from industry and academia for interviews. Only one of our participants reported that reproducibility is a priority during development, effectively confirming our technical findings. Beyond technical barriers (e.g., timestamps included in the binary) that can be readily addressed, we identify broader ecosystem-level challenges, such as the lack of control over the build environment in projects involving multiple stakeholders. We argue that achieving reproducibility in TEEs requires a holistic development approach that extends beyond individual developers and calls for stronger commitments - rather than treating TEEs as a "security badge".

</details>


### [64] [Heterogeneous Cross-Chain Transaction Tracing for Solana Bridges via Candidate-Set Selective Decision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11413)
*Wenjie Dou,Zheng Che,Meng Shen,Hanbiao Du,Qing Li,Yan Qiang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 该论文针对跨链资金进入 Solana 后难以追踪的问题，提出 SolTracer，通过统一不同链与 Solana 的执行语义，并在候选集中选择性决策，实现更可靠的跨账本交易关联。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Solana 与 EVM 链在执行模型、日志机制和交易语义上差异明显，现有跨链追踪方法难以直接迁移；同时，跨链桥被频繁用于混淆资金来源，使得 Solana-bound 跨链交易关联成为一个具有现实价值但技术难度较高的问题。

Method: 论文首先形式化了四类流向 Solana 的跨链交易模式；随后将不同链和桥机制中的异构执行语义映射到统一事件空间；最后使用候选集选择性决策机制，在存在有效目标时进行关联，在目标缺失时主动拒判，以适配闭集、开集和跨源链泛化等场景。

Result: 实验显示 SolTracer 在闭集关联、开集关联和跨源链泛化三类代表性任务中均优于现有 SOTA 方法。尤其在开放世界设定下，当 TA 比例为 50% 时，SolTracer 相比最强基线的 F1 分数提升 20.16%。基于该方法的实证分析还揭示了不同桥机制在交易数量与金额上的显著分化、跨资产转换的普遍性，以及链上结算与区块浏览器可见性之间的脱钩。

Conclusion: 该工作的重要性在于填补了 Solana 跨链追踪中的语义鸿沟，将跨链交易关联从简单规则匹配推进到面向异构执行语义的选择性判别框架。摘要体现出较强的问题针对性和实证价值，但完整评价仍需关注数据集构建、候选集生成方式、真实标签可靠性以及在更复杂桥路由和多跳交易中的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Solana is a rapidly growing high-throughput blockchain platform that has attracted substantial liquidity and user activity. However, this expansion has also drawn the attention of illicit actors, who frequently leverage cross-chain bridges to route illicit funds onto Solana to obfuscate transaction lineage. Unlike EVM-compatible platforms, Solana features distinct execution dynamics and lacks standard event logs, creating severe semantic gaps that prevent existing tracing methods from reliably correlating cross-ledger transactions. In this paper, we formalize four types of Solana-bound cross-chain transaction modes and propose a candidate-set selective decision-based tracing method called SolTracer. SolTracer maps disparate execution semantics into a unified event space and employs candidate-set selective decision-making to reliably associate target transactions while abstaining when valid targets are absent. Extensive experiments demonstrate that SolTracer outperforms state-of-the-art (SOTA) methods across three representative scenarios: closed-world association, open-world association, and cross-source-chain generalization. In particular, under the challenging open-world setting with a 50% TA ratio, SolTracer improves the F1 score by 20.16% over the strongest SOTA baseline. Utilizing SolTracer, we conduct an empirical analysis on real-world cross-chain transfers to investigate ecosystem dynamics. Our analysis explores the stark count-value divergence across bridge mechanisms, the prevalence of cross-asset shifts, and the decoupling between on-chain settlement and explorer visibility.

</details>


### [65] [Chypothermia: Clock Freezing for Static Side-channel Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11442)
*Fatemeh Khojasteh Dana,Mehmet Ali Cetin,Xinrui Wang,Andrew Butler,Yuval Yarom,Shahin Tajik*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出 Chypothermia：通过低温干扰芯片片上混合信号模块，使时钟/电压/温度相关防护传感器失效，从而绕过静态侧信道防护并保留敏感数据；同时提出 FPGA 兼容的自加热传感器作为防御。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 静态侧信道攻击利用停钟条件读取敏感信息，现有防御通常依赖时钟异常检测并触发密钥清零。但如果检测链路本身依赖易受环境条件影响的混合信号电路，则攻击者可能无需电气篡改即可使防护机制失灵。

Method: 作者利用低温暴露影响片上传感与信号生成组件，分别使目标时钟传感器、时钟生成电路和电压传感器失效，同时避免触发数据清零。由于单纯冷却速度慢且可能被温度传感器发现，论文进一步将 Chypothermia 与 Chypnosis 结合，在中等低温范围内实现停钟并规避检测。实验覆盖多个 FPGA/SoC 平台、软 IP 与硬 IP 传感器，并验证 OpenTitan alert handler 场景。

Result: 实验表明，该方法可成功禁用不同实现形态的传感器，绕过 OpenTitan 中先进时钟传感器的检测，并阻止密钥 zeroization。论文还实现了 FPGA 兼容的自加热传感器，显示其可抵御该低温失效路径。

Conclusion: 该工作揭示了环境温度对芯片安全监测电路的系统性威胁：仅依赖片上传感器进行异常检测并不充分。其贡献在于提出一种非电气篡改型低温攻击、验证其跨平台可行性，并给出可实现的自加热检测防护方案；局限在于抽象中未充分说明攻击时间、温控精度、物理接触条件及大规模量产芯片适用性。

Abstract: Static side-channel attacks, which exploit halted-clock conditions to extract sensitive information, pose an increasing threat to chip security. To counter these attacks, various defenses have been proposed that monitor for abnormal clock behavior and trigger the clearing of sensitive data when clock anomalies are detected. In this work, we demonstrate that exposing a chip to cryogenic temperatures interferes with on-chip mixed-signal components, responsible for signal sensing and generation. Based on this observation, we develop Chypothermia, an attack that, without any electrical tampering with the system, disables the target clock sensor, the clock generation circuit, and the voltage sensors, while preserving the secret data.
  While effective at halting the clock, cooling is a slow process and, on its own, is often insufficient against systems equipped with temperature sensors designed to detect thermal anomalies. To bypass these protections, we combine Chypothermia with Chypnosis (Mitard et al., IEEE SP 2026) and show that, even within a moderately low-temperature operating range, this combination can halt the clock while evading detection. We implement Chypothermia on multiple FPGA/SoC platforms and demonstrate successful disabling of both soft-IP and hard-IP sensor implementations. Finally, we apply Chypothermia to the alert handler of the OpenTitan root of trust, which incorporates a state-of-the-art clock sensor, and show that the attack evades detection and prevents key zeroization. Finally, we introduce and implement an FPGA-compatible self-heating sensor as a countermeasure and demonstrate its robustness against Chypothermia.

</details>


### [66] [On Identifying Sound Conditions for Frontrunning Resistance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11535)
*Sebastian Holler,Anna Piscitelli,Jannik Albrecht,Stephan Dübler,Ghassan Karame,Clara Schneidewind*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文针对智能合约中的抢先交易问题提出首个形式化漏洞定义，并给出可综合安全交互条件的健全算法；实验表明，既有动态检测标准遗漏了大量已审计报告中的相关问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 区块链交易的异步排序使出块节点能够重排、延迟或插入交易，从而在用户之前获得不当优势。现有研究缺少严格的安全定义，导致检测工具的覆盖范围和结果解释均受到限制。

Method: 作者将安全性建模为合约行为与诚实用户交互方式共同决定的性质，而非合约自身的孤立属性。在此基础上，提出抢先交易漏洞的形式化定义，设计用于合成交互约束的健全算法，并实现原型工具应用于真实审计合约。

Result: 对287份智能合约审计报告、393项已报告漏洞的统计显示，55%的问题不满足现有主流动态检测准则，说明这些准则存在系统性遗漏。原型还在两个以太坊合约中发现此前未被识别的漏洞。

Conclusion: 论文的核心贡献是将抢先交易防护从单纯的合约代码属性扩展为“合约—用户交互”联合安全属性。该形式化框架与条件合成方法为更准确地评估和构造抗抢先交易的智能合约交互流程提供了基础。

Abstract: Blockchains enable decentralized applications through smart contracts---interactive programs executed through consensus. However, the inherently asynchronous nature of blockchain transaction ordering introduces a class of vulnerabilities known as frontrunning attacks, which have caused millions of dollars in losses in major blockchains, such as Ethereum. Frontrunning attacks arise because users interact with smart contracts through transactions, which are added to the blockchain by designated nodes called miners. Miners can exploit their ability to reorder, delay, or insert transactions to gain an advantage over honest users, effectively frontrunning them.
  Yet, to date, the field lacks a rigorous definition of what it even means for a contract to resist such attacks. Worse, we show that existing dynamic detection approaches are fundamentally inadequate: in a large-scale study comprising 287 smart contract audits, 55% of the 393 reported vulnerabilities identified by leading smart contract auditors fall outside the scope of state-of-the-art detection criteria. To address this gap, we propose the first formal definition of frontrunning vulnerability for smart contracts. Our definition captures a key insight: resistance to frontrunning is not an intrinsic property of a contract alone, but depends critically on how honest users interact with it. Grounded in this observation, we develop a sound algorithm for synthesizing secure interaction conditions, alongside a prototype implementation that we apply to audited real-world contracts---revealing previously undiscovered vulnerabilities in two Ethereum contracts.

</details>


### [67] [Accountability in Certificate Transparency and Variants](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11552)
*Timo Treitz,Robert Künnemann*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 该论文将证书透明性（CT）的核心目标重新界定为“可问责性”，并在 Dolev-Yao 符号模型中系统分析从传统 PKI 到 CT 及其扩展机制的安全性质。结论是：基础 CT 仍依赖诚实日志，但在该假设下可实现问责；SCT Auditing 可去除此诚实日志假设，而 Gossiping 不能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CT 旨在降低 TLS 证书生态中对证书颁发机构（CA）的信任，但其实际安全性依赖 CA、日志、监控方和客户端之间复杂的交互。既有形式化分析往往模型过度简化，且安全定义偏向捕捉 CT 的设计特征，而非其真正目标。因此，论文希望给出更准确的目标刻画与形式化分析。
