Method: 论文采用 Dolev-Yao 模型进行逐步建模与验证：先分析基础 PKI，再逐步引入 CT 机制，最后分析两个扩展方案 SCT Auditing 与 Gossiping。其核心安全目标不是传统意义上的完全防止错误证书，而是当错误或不一致行为发生时，能否将责任归因到具体参与方。

Result: 研究表明，基础 CT 并不能完全消除信任假设，而是将关键假设转移到日志的诚实性上；在日志诚实的前提下，CT 能提供可问责性。SCT Auditing 扩展通过客户端或审计机制检查 SCT 与日志行为，可以去除对诚实日志的依赖。相比之下，Gossiping 虽然有助于传播和比较日志视图，但不足以单独消除该信任假设。

Conclusion: 论文的主要贡献在于把 CT 的安全目标从“消除信任”澄清为“实现问责”，并形式化区分基础 CT 与扩展机制的能力边界。其结论强调：CT 的价值不在于完全避免恶意证书或恶意参与方，而在于通过公开日志与审计机制使违规行为可检测、可归责。

Abstract: Certificate Transparency (CT) aims to reduce the trust required in Certificate Authorities (CAs) within the TLS certificate ecosystem. It is supported by all major browsers. The protocol obliges all CAs to record the certificates they issue in a public log, which itself is monitored for compliance and consistency by third parties. Given this complex set of checks between the four roles-CA, loggers, monitor but also the end user's client-it is very hard to provide a precise account of how CT eliminates trust assumptions in exchange for complex infrastructure. Analyses both in the Dolev-Yao paradigm and the computational paradigm only regard a very simplified model and feature definitions adapted specifically to CAs, essentially capturing design features rather than the target property. The present paper posits accountability as the main goal of CT and presents a thorough analysis in the Dolev-Yao model. We start with the vanilla PKI and, step by step, move to CT, finally analyzing proposed extensions for SCT Auditing and Gossiping. We show that plain CT relies on an honest log, but provides accountability under this assumption. Furthermore, we show that the SCT Auditing extension can eliminate this assumption, while the Gossiping extension cannot.

</details>


### [68] [From Intent to Execution Grant: An Execution-Boundary Conformance Profile for High-Risk AI Actions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11596)
*Mengting Wu,Lin Wang,Yong Zhang,Jiang Deng*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出 EBL-Core，一种面向 AI 生成候选动作的执行边界一致性规范，用于在明确条件下判断某个完整候选动作是否可以获得限定于该动作的执行权限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有授权引擎、策略语言、运行时监控、溯源机制和代理护栏虽能提供基础能力，但通常缺少从具体候选动作到最终执行授权之间的统一语义契约，因此需要一个可验证、可复现且与执行授权解耦的边界规范。

Method: EBL-Core 通过执行释放契约（ERC）绑定结构化意图对象、根策略与操作策略、证据义务、类型化证据、上下文、时间信息及可验证的决策推导。规范涵盖动作绑定、策略不可弱化、证据处理、确定性裁决、推导验证和授权生命周期；参考实现包含模式定义、裁决逻辑、独立验证、语义重放以及线性化内存授权存储。ERC 本身不承载执行权限，验证通过的 ALLOW ERC 还需在兑换时接受独立校验后才能形成执行授权。

Result: 参考实现中，34 个静态测试向量和 15 个生命周期检查均得到预期结果。在 100 次并发测试中，每次 32 个 Redemption 请求均恰好只有一次成功并触发受保护测试效果；100 次撤销与兑换竞态测试均进入有效终态。

Conclusion: 研究证明了 EBL-Core 指定子集及其参考实现的可执行性、一致性和基本并发安全性，尤其验证了单次兑换和撤销竞态处理。但结果不等同于人类意图正确、证据真实、完整仲裁、生产就绪、形式化正确性或部署级安全性。

Abstract: AI agents increasingly propose actions with external consequences, including financial transfers, infrastructure changes, software deployments, disclosures, and physical actuation. Authorization engines, policy languages, runtime monitors, provenance mechanisms, and agent guardrails provide important foundations, but do not necessarily define a common semantic contract for the final transition from a particular candidate action to execution authority.
  We specify EBL-Core, an execution-boundary conformance profile for deciding whether one canonical, fully materialized AI-generated candidate may receive action-scoped execution authority under explicit conditions. It binds a structured intent object, Root and Operational Policies, evidence obligations, typed evidence, context, time, and a verifiable Decision Derivation through an Execution Release Contract (ERC). An ERC is not an authority-bearing token; a verified ALLOW ERC may support a separate Execution Grant governed by Redemption-time validation.
  EBL-Core specifies action binding, policy non-weakening, evidence handling, deterministic adjudication, derivation verification, and grant lifecycle behavior. An accompanying reference artifact provides schemas, adjudication, separate verification and Semantic Replay, and a linearizable in-memory grant store. In the retained run, 34 static vectors and 15 lifecycle checks matched expected outcomes. Across 100 trials, 32 concurrent Redemption attempts yielded exactly one successful Redemption and protected test effect per trial; 100 Revoke-Redeem races ended in valid terminal outcomes. These bounded results demonstrate executability of the specified subset, not human-intent correctness, evidence truth, complete mediation, production readiness, mechanized correctness, or deployment-level security.

</details>


### [69] [PHAT: PHotonic Accelerator for TFHE](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11613)
*Guowei Yang,Farbin Fayza,Beren Aydoğan,Carlos A. Ríos Ocampo,Ayse K. Coskun,Ajay Joshi*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出 PHAT：一种面向 TFHE 的光子加速器，利用 OPCM 处理存储一体架构加速 bootstrapping 中的 FFT 瓶颈，相比先进 ASIC 加速器在四个实际工作负载上实现 2.14–5.10 倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TFHE 支持灵活的同态运算，但 bootstrapping 阶段需要大量 FFT，导致计算与通信开销极高。传统电子加速器受制于工艺缩放放缓和存储墙问题，难以继续显著提升吞吐。

Method: 核心方法是将 FFT 映射到基于 OPCM 的电光混合处理存储一体系统。作者设计了 OPCM-based FFT 单元、适配 OPCM 特性的 twiddle-stationary 数据流，以及提高 FFT 单元利用率的调度机制，用于缓解模拟计算精度要求高、OPCM 编程延迟和能耗高等问题。

Result: 实验结果显示，PHAT 在四个真实 TFHE 工作负载上相对当前先进 ASIC 加速器取得 2.14×–5.10× 的速度提升，表明光子 PIM 架构能够有效缓解 TFHE 中 FFT 密集型计算的性能瓶颈。

Conclusion: 该工作的重要性在于将光子计算和 OPCM 存内计算引入 TFHE 加速，并针对 FFT 的数据流和调度进行架构级优化。其主要贡献偏系统架构与数据流设计；后续仍需进一步关注模拟误差、OPCM 寿命、面积/能耗细节、可制造性以及在更大规模 FHE 应用中的稳定性。

Abstract: Fully Homomorphic Encryption (FHE) enables secure computation on encrypted data, making it a promising solution for privacy-preserving applications in the cloud. Among various FHE schemes, FHE over the Torus (TFHE) stands out due to its support for arbitrary operations. However, its high computation and communication overhead, particularly in the Fast Fourier Transform (FFT) operations required during bootstrapping, limits its practicality for real-world applications. Conventional electronic accelerators struggle to achieve sufficient throughput due to the limitations of technology scaling and the memory-wall problem.
  To address these challenges, we propose PHAT, a PHotonic Accelerator for TFHE leveraging Optically-addressed Phase-Change Memory (OPCM). OPCM-based processing-in-memory systems offer high computation and communication throughput, making them well-suited for accelerating FFT operations in TFHE. However, directly mapping FFT to OPCM presents challenges such as high-precision analog computation and the high latency and energy cost of programming OPCM cells. To overcome these challenges, we introduce a novel electro-photonic accelerator architecture optimized for TFHE, featuring OPCM-based FFT units, a twiddle-stationary dataflow tailored for OPCM, and a scheduling mechanism to maximize the utilization of the FFT units. PHAT delivers $2.14\times$--$5.10\times$ speedup across four real-world TFHE workloads against the state-of-the-art ASIC accelerator. Our approach significantly enhances the performance of TFHE applications, paving the way for practical and efficient homomorphic encryption in cloud computing.

</details>


### [70] [Signing the Transaction but Not the Decision: Whisper Attacks and a Binding Defense for AP2](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11757)
*Yedidel Louck,Amit Dvir,Ariel Stulman*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文揭示：AP2等代理支付协议虽能验证签名，却无法约束购物代理形成支付意图的过程，导致普通商品描述即可诱导代理执行未授权操作。作者提出A-VIP，通过会话、商品列表与支出绑定缓解该风险，并发布代码、形式化不变量和1,544个测试场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有代理支付协议主要验证交易完成后的密码学有效性，却缺乏对代理决策链和用户原始意图的一致性约束。攻击者可利用商品描述操纵代理获取不应访问的支付凭证、生成与展示内容不一致的购物车，或诱导其选择更昂贵的商品。

Method: 作者设计并验证三类攻击：诱导代理获取其他会话的支付凭证；构造密码学有效但与用户所见内容不一致的购物车；通过关于库存或产品来源的单一事实陈述，将代理从低价展示商品引向高价商品。实验覆盖Gemini Flash-Lite、17个Google模型、3种独立代理框架、2个跨厂商锚点及Google消费级助手。随后提出A-VIP，将签名意图视为能力授权，并将凭证查询绑定到请求会话、将购物车行绑定到所见商品列表，同时检测未经授权的支出。

Result: 三类攻击成功率分别为90%、56%和73.3%。该漏洞在不同模型、框架和助手中均出现。A-VIP可通过结构绑定以零误报阻断前两类攻击；第三类攻击不会留下可检测的结构痕迹，因此系统将未经授权的支出提交用户确认。作者还发布了A-VIP实现、机器检查不变量和包含1,544个场景的AP2-WhisperBench。

Conclusion: 密码学签名只能证明交易在协议层面有效，不能证明交易符合用户意图。代理支付系统需要在协议层绑定会话、展示内容与授权支出。A-VIP能有效处理具有结构性证据的攻击，并将难以静态判定的意图偏移转化为用户确认问题，从而提升代理支付的可审计性与安全性。

Abstract: Software agents are beginning to shop and pay on a person's behalf. Agent payment protocols such as AP2 produce cryptographically valid signatures for completed purchases, yet do not constrain the decisions that lead to them. Consequently, ordinary product-description text can steer a shopping agent into forming a cart that passes every protocol check but no longer matches the user's request. In this paper, we show that this vulnerability enables three related attacks. In the first attack, the agent is steered into fetching another user's payment credentials. In the second, it assembles a cryptographically valid cart whose contents do not match what the user was shown. In the third, a single factual claim about stock or product lineage moves the agent from the cheaper displayed item to a more expensive one, while the resulting cart remains fully consistent with the listing. In experiments using the Gemini Flash-Lite models that AP2's sample agents specify by default, the three attacks succeeded at rates of 90%, 56%, and 73.3%, respectively. The same vulnerability appears across seventeen Google models, three unrelated agent frameworks, two cross-vendor anchors, and Google's own consumer assistant. To address this attack vector, we introduce A-VIP (AP2 Verified-Intent Protection), a protocol-layer defense that treats the signed intent as a capability grant rather than judging the merchant's description. The defense binds every credential lookup to the session that requested it and every cart line to the listing seen, while flagging unauthorized spending. The first two attacks leave structural traces that these bindings block with zero false positives. The third attack leaves no trace, so A-VIP surfaces unauthorized spending for user confirmation. Finally, we release the A-VIP code, machine-checked invariants, and AP2-WhisperBench, a suite of 1,544 evaluation scenarios.

</details>


### [71] [Differentially Private EEG Feature Anonymization: A Privacy-Utility Case Study in Clinical Neurophysiology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11777)
*Noman Sadiq,Mohsen Toorani*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究如何利用差分隐私保护临床脑电图（EEG）特征表示，并比较高斯扰动与拉普拉斯扰动在不同部署场景下的隐私—效用权衡。结果表明，差分隐私具有可行性，但噪声机制、隐私参数和敏感度校准会显著影响数据质量及下游任务性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床EEG及其派生特征可能包含可识别患者身份或推断个体信息，传统去标识化方法难以应对高维生物医学信号中的重识别、关联和推断风险。同时，过强的隐私保护会破坏具有临床意义的信号特征，因此需要评估隐私保护强度与数据实用性之间的平衡。

Method: 研究在EEG预处理和特征提取后，以患者为单位对特征表示施加高斯或拉普拉斯扰动。框架覆盖客户端匿名化、集中式服务器端匿名化和去中心化本地训练三种部署场景。研究使用统计效用指标和基于机器学习的下游效用评估扰动影响；拉普拉斯实验分别考察实际实现的噪声尺度，并单独推导完整向量形式化校准所需的噪声尺度。

Result: 差分隐私扰动可以被整合进临床EEG处理流程，但不同扰动机制、隐私参数和敏感度校准方式会造成明显不同的效用损失。研究进一步指出，小规模且类别不平衡的临床EEG数据集对噪声更加敏感，统计指标与下游机器学习性能均可能受到影响。

Conclusion: 基于差分隐私的EEG特征匿名化能够降低患者隐私风险，但其实际价值取决于隐私预算、噪声机制和敏感度估计的合理设计。对于临床应用，应在保护强度与信号可用性之间进行针对任务的数据集级权衡，并特别关注小样本和类别不平衡条件下的下游性能保持。

Abstract: Clinical electroencephalography (EEG) data are valuable for healthcare research and for developing artificial intelligence (AI)-based clinical decision-support systems, but EEG recordings and derived features may contain sensitive patient-specific information. This creates privacy risks when data are reused, analyzed, or shared across clinical and research environments. Conventional anonymization methods are often insufficient for high-dimensional biomedical signals, since removing direct identifiers does not necessarily prevent re-identification, linkage, or inference risks. At the same time, strong privacy protection may distort clinically relevant signal characteristics and reduce data utility. This paper studies subject-level differential privacy for protecting clinical EEG-derived feature representations using Gaussian and Laplace perturbations. The proposed framework considers three deployment scenarios: client-side anonymization, centralized server-side anonymization, and decentralized local training. Following EEG preprocessing and feature extraction, Gaussian and Laplace perturbations are applied to the resulting patient-level EEG feature representations. The Laplace experiments evaluate the implemented noise scales, while the scales required for formal full-vector calibration are derived separately. The effects of both perturbations are assessed using statistical utility measures and a downstream machine-learning-based utility check. The results show that differentially private perturbation can be integrated into EEG processing workflows, but the selected mechanism, privacy parameters, and sensitivity calibration strongly influence data utility. The study highlights the practical privacy-utility trade-off in DP-based EEG feature anonymization and the challenges of preserving downstream utility in small and imbalanced clinical EEG datasets.

</details>


### [72] [SpecGuard: Inference-Time Backdoor Detection For Free](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11799)
*Rui Wen,Ahmed Salem,Andrew Paverd,Mark Russinovich,Zheng Li*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出SpecGuard，一种利用推测解码过程中已有的草稿令牌接受率变化来进行运行时后门检测的方法，无需额外模型计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有推理时后门检测器通常依赖特定触发器形式，难以识别隐蔽攻击；或需要输入扰动、额外生成等计算开销，难以满足大语言模型服务的低延迟要求。对于频繁更新或来源复杂的模型，部署后的持续监控仍然必要。

Method: SpecGuard复用推测解码机制：小型草稿模型先生成候选令牌，目标模型随后验证这些令牌。正常情况下，草稿模型与目标模型的预测较为一致；触发后门时，目标模型转向攻击行为，而干净的草稿模型不会同步转变，从而导致草稿令牌接受率发生变化。论文形式化分析了该检测信号出现的条件，并论证抑制该信号会削弱后门效果。

Result: 在多种后门类型和模型系列上的实验表明，SpecGuard能够可靠检测已触发的后门行为，包括输入级过滤器难以识别的隐蔽攻击，同时避免额外生成过程和模型计算开销。摘要未提供具体的检测率、误报率或延迟数值。

Conclusion: 推测解码不仅能够加速推理，还可作为持续、低成本的运行时后门检测信号。SpecGuard在不增加模型计算的前提下提升了部署阶段的安全监控能力，但其实际效果仍取决于草稿模型的干净程度、接受率变化的可分辨性及实验中攻击类型的覆盖范围。

Abstract: Large language models are often fine-tuned, shared, or downloaded from third parties, so a deployed model may carry a hidden backdoor that behaves normally on benign inputs but switches to attacker-controlled behavior when a secret trigger appears. While backdoors can be audited before deployment, runtime monitoring remains important for models that are frequently updated. The challenge is that LLM serving is latency-sensitive: existing inference-time detectors either rely on assumptions about the trigger form, which can fail on stealthy attacks, or require extra model computation, such as input perturbations or an additional generation pass.
  We introduce SpecGuard, an inference-time backdoor detector that repurposes speculative decoding at zero added model-computation cost. Speculative decoding speeds up inference by using a small draft model to propose tokens and a target model to verify them. We observe that this verification process already exposes a useful signal: when a backdoor is triggered, the target model shifts toward the attacker's behavior, while a clean draft model does not predict this shift, causing the draft-token acceptance rate to change.
  We formalize when this signal appears and show that an attacker who suppresses it must also weaken the backdoor. Across diverse backdoor types and model families, SpecGuard reliably detects triggered behavior, including stealthy cases where input-level filters are blind, while avoiding the extra generation cost of existing runtime detectors. Speculative decoding therefore doubles as a free, always-on signal for detecting backdoored LLM behavior.

</details>


### [73] [Don't Trust the Super-App: A Case Study of Russia's Max](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11814)
*Richa Priyanka,Aaron Ortwein,Joel Reardon,Michael Specter,Piyush Kumar Sharma,Roya Ensafi*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.

Method: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.

Result: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.

Conclusion: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.

Abstract: Super-apps, an emerging mobile architecture, host third-party mini-apps inside a single app, allowing users to access diverse services. A decade of security research on the super-app ecosystem has all assumed super-apps to be a trusted intermediary. We argue this implicit trust is difficult to justify: China's WeChat is already shown to passively track its user's activity across mini-apps at extraordinary scale; Russia's MAX's parent company is reported to be deeply entangled with the state prosecution of online speech; and Iran's Bale was reported to be functioning in the world's longest internet shutdown due to its state-backed support.
  In this paper, we show how malicious super-apps have undeniable capabilities to silently undermine the security and privacy of mini-apps and users without leaving any trace. Using MAX as an example, we show how it can capture mini-app UI, read and write mini-app local storage, inject arbitrary JavaScript into a mini-app's runtime, mediate mini-app network traffic, and control authentication context in ways that can enable silent user impersonation. Sadly, these capabilities manifest themselves in any super-app because of the architectural privileges granted to them by design. We argue that mobile OS and app store interventions are urgently needed to close this architectural blind spot before it is further exploited.

</details>


### [74] [Atlas: Efficient Verifiable Semantic Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11841)
*Nikolay Avramov,Hidde Lycklama,Alexander Viand,Anwar Hithnawi*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: Atlas提出一种面向HNSW语义搜索的零知识证明系统，使服务提供者能够证明查询结果确实来自约定算法和已承诺索引，同时不泄露索引内容。系统在SIFT1M上可在1秒内完成证明，在1亿向量规模下耗时2.0秒，并保持明文HNSW的召回率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语义搜索由服务提供者控制索引和执行过程，客户端无法确认结果是否来自正确算法和完整索引。提供者可能为降低成本而截断搜索或偏置结果。已有可验证检索方案主要采用规则化、基于聚类的索引，牺牲了图搜索方法HNSW的召回性能；而HNSW的数据依赖遍历也难以适配零知识证明中的固定约束系统。

Method: Atlas围绕HNSW搜索构建零知识证明，包含三项技术：首先，将所有依赖数据库的计算转移至离线预处理，使在线证明成本主要随实际遍历规模增长；其次，将HNSW重构为固定状态大小的过程，并证明该过程与原始搜索返回相同结果；最后，为每个遍历步骤引入时间步标签，通过批处理将整个遍历的多个局部证明合并为一个证明。

Result: Atlas在SIFT1M基准上可在1秒内证明查询，在1亿向量规模下证明耗时2.0秒，同时保持明文HNSW的召回率，并且除查询结果外不泄露索引信息。在完整的检索增强生成流程中，Atlas保持了端到端回答质量，其回答质量高于既有可验证检索系统，同时证明成本更低。

Conclusion: Atlas首次实现了大规模可验证的图结构语义搜索，消除了客户端对搜索执行过程的信任假设。其核心设计使HNSW的高召回优势能够与零知识证明的正确性和隐私保护结合，适用于需要可信检索的生产级语义搜索和检索增强生成系统。

Abstract: Semantic search is a core primitive of modern applications, powering recommender systems, web search, and retrieval-augmented generation for language models. The provider controls the index and query execution, leaving clients to trust that results come from the right algorithm over the intended index. A provider may truncate search to cut cost, bias results, or otherwise deviate from the specified execution undetected. Verifiability can remove this trust assumption by proving that results follow the agreed algorithm over a committed index. Realizing this efficiently is hard, as retrieval at scale relies on HNSW, a graph-based algorithm whose data-dependent traversal maps poorly onto the fixed constraint systems of zero-knowledge proofs. Prior verifiable systems therefore target regular, cluster-based indices that are easier to encode, sacrificing the recall of graph-based search. We present Atlas, a system that lets a provider prove a query was answered correctly against its committed index without revealing the index. At its core is a new zero-knowledge proof for HNSW search, built on three techniques: preprocessing that shifts all database-dependent cost offline, so per-query proving scales with the traversal rather than the database; a restructuring of HNSW into a fixed-size-state procedure that we prove returns the same result; and a timestep-tagged batching that merges the per-step arguments of the entire traversal into one. Atlas is the first to demonstrate verifiable graph-based search at scale, proving a query in under a second on the SIFT1M benchmark and in 2.0 seconds at 100 million vectors, while maintaining the recall of plaintext HNSW and revealing nothing about the index beyond the result. In a complete RAG pipeline, Atlas' proven retrieval preserves end-to-end answer quality, and reaches higher quality at lower proving cost than all prior verifiable retrieval systems.

</details>


### [75] [BlueSTAR: Tiered Agentic Architecture for Autonomous Cyber Defense](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11852)
*Simona Boboila,Xavier Cadet,Edward Koh,Daniel Balasubramanian,Dirk Van Bruggen,Peter Chin,Alina Oprea*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出BlueSTAR，一种面向企业IT/OT网络的分层智能体架构，通过压缩安全遥测、结合上下文推理与受控响应，实现对已知和未知攻击链的自主防御。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动化网络攻击压缩了人工分析与响应时间，而原始日志具有高吞吐、语义歧义和异构性，直接使用大语言模型处理全部遥测既不现实，也可能因不受约束的行动带来运行风险。因此，需要兼顾处理效率、推理能力和防御行动安全性的架构。

Method: BlueSTAR采用分层智能体设计：首先将高容量安全遥测转换为紧凑的失陷指标，以降低模型处理负担；随后利用大语言模型进行上下文关联、跨周期推理和响应决策。同时提出韧性指标，联合衡量攻击者可达范围、对关键任务资产的影响，以及防御行动造成的业务扰动。系统在两个实时企业IT/OT网络靶场中，基于七条源自真实入侵技术的攻击链进行评估。

Result: 在不同攻击链上，BlueSTAR保持了确定性响应机制对已知威胁的快速遏制能力，并能够处理需要上下文和跨周期推理的复杂场景，包括凭证窃取、重复入侵、并发攻击者以及针对物理过程的攻击。摘要未给出具体检测率、响应时间、误报率或韧性指标数值。

Conclusion: 研究表明，分层遥测压缩、智能推理和受控行动相结合，能够提升LLM驱动自主防御在企业IT/OT环境中的可行性。BlueSTAR在保持已知威胁快速响应的同时，扩展了对复杂、持续和跨域攻击链的防御能力，但其定量优势、泛化能力及真实生产环境中的运行成本仍需进一步验证。

Abstract: Cyber attacks are increasingly automated, narrowing the time available for human analysts to detect, reason about, and respond to intrusions. Large language models (LLMs) offer a promising foundation for autonomous cyber defense because they can correlate heterogeneous evidence and reason about previously unseen threats. However, directly applying LLMs to operational security telemetry is impractical: raw logs arrive faster than current models can process them, individual events are often ambiguous, and unconstrained LLM actions can introduce significant operational risk. We present BlueSTAR, a tiered agentic architecture for autonomous cyber defense in enterprise IT/OT networks. BlueSTAR first transforms high-volume security telemetry into compact indicators of compromise. We further introduce a resilience metric that jointly captures attacker reach, impact on mission-critical assets, and disruption caused by defensive actions. We evaluate BlueSTAR on two live enterprise IT/OT cyber ranges using seven attack chains based on real-world intrusion techniques. Across attack chains, BlueSTAR retains the fast containment of deterministic response for known threats while successfully defending against attacks requiring contextual and cross-cycle reasoning, including credential theft, repeated compromise, concurrent attackers, and attacks against physical processes.

</details>


### [76] [From Specs to Apps: Verifying and Monitoring Models of Signal and WhatsApp](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11882)
*Moustafa Said,Aurora Naska,Kevin Morio,Robert Künnemann*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文将运行时监控工具 SpecMon 应用于真实通信应用中的 Signal 协议实现，验证 WhatsApp Web 与 Signal Desktop 的实际执行是否符合形式化模型，并发现实现层面的未文档化差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有研究主要证明 Signal 协议规范在形式化模型中的安全性，但规范安全并不等同于真实应用运行时行为安全。论文关注协议模型与实际实现之间的语义落差，试图验证真实执行是否仍满足认证与机密性等核心安全性质。

Method: 作者对 WhatsApp Web 和 Signal Desktop 进行插桩，捕获网络交互与密码组件事件；基于这些事件构建两个兼容 Tamarin 的多重集重写模型；随后使用 SpecMon 对真实执行轨迹进行一致性监控，并结合 fuzzing 与故障注入评估检测能力和开销。

Result: 论文给出了首个 WhatsApp Web 中 Signal 协议实现的形式化模型，以及迄今较详细的 Signal 原始协议模型。监控结果表明，在可信事件提取与符号抽象假设下，观测执行与模型一致；核心协议组件通过认证与机密性验证；实验还揭示了 libsignal 原版与 WhatsApp 分支之间此前未公开的实现差异。

Conclusion: 该工作的重要贡献在于把形式化验证从协议规范推进到真实实现运行时，为大规模真实应用的协议一致性检查提供了可复现方法。其价值主要体现在工程可行性、低监控开销和故障检测能力；局限在于结论依赖事件提取可信性和符号抽象精度，不能完全替代对底层实现与平台环境的端到端安全审计。

Abstract: The Signal protocol is a prominent messaging protocol that secures communication for billions of users. It powers WhatsApp, the most widely used messaging application worldwide, and the Signal app, popular among privacy-conscious users. Extensive research in the computational and Dolev-Yao settings provides strong formal security guarantees for the protocol itself. However, a gap remains between the guarantees of the protocol specification and the implementation's actual behavior at runtime. In this work, we bridge this gap by applying SpecMon, a recently proposed runtime monitor, to check whether observed executions conform to formal protocol models. To this end, we instrument two applications (WhatsApp Web and Signal Desktop) to capture their interactions with the network and the cryptographic components. Using this instrumentation, we develop two multiset-rewrite models that are compatible with Tamarin, thus enabling verification. We derive the first model of WhatsApp Web's implementation of the Signal protocol and the most detailed model to date of Signal's original protocol. Monitoring establishes that observed executions conform to these models, relative to the trusted event extraction and the symbolic abstraction. For the core components of the Signal protocol, we verify authentication and secrecy properties. Finally, monitoring reveals previously undocumented differences between the original libsignal library and WhatsApp's fork. We evaluate our methodology and demonstrate its reproducibility. Developing the WhatsApp Web model, instrumenting the app, adding fuzzing, and running the experiments took three person-weeks. We also demonstrate efficient monitoring of real-world applications and detection of deliberately injected security faults, with low overhead in our measured setting.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [77] [Probabilistic Focal Search: Accelerating Bounded-Suboptimal Search via Lower-Bound Advancement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10584)
*Minh Vu Duc,Trung Le Huu,Hà Minh Hoàng,Trung Thanh Nguyen,Phuong Khanh Nguyen,Huynh Thi Thanh Binh*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出概率焦点搜索（PFS），以概率p采用FOCAL中的启发式选择，以概率1-p选择最小f值的OPEN节点，从而加速f_min推进并扩大FOCAL。实验表明，在长f_min平台导致FOCAL延迟纳入有效节点时，PFS可显著减少搜索开销，节点扩展量最高降低约90%或更多。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统焦点搜索（FS）的确定性选择可能连续扩展大量具有相同f_min的节点，使下界长期不变，进而延迟有潜力节点进入FOCAL并增加获得有界次优解的时间。研究旨在平衡启发式引导与下界推进，缓解FOCAL准入成为瓶颈的问题。

Method: PFS以概率p执行FS在FOCAL中的引导选择，以概率1-p扩展OPEN中f值最小的节点。该调度机制进一步迁移到动态势搜索，形成概率动态势搜索（PDPS）。研究在N-Puzzle、Pancake Sorting和旅行商问题上比较PFS与FS，并在广义覆盖旅行商问题上评估其任意时扩展APFS，测试多个w和p参数。

Result: PFS在存在长f_min平台的N-Puzzle和TSP上收益最大，节点扩展量可减少约90%或更多；在确定性搜索已能高效推进的Pancake Sorting上，改进较小。APFS在GCTSP的任意时算法评估中优于所有测试算法。PDPS也显示出机制可迁移至势函数引导，但效果依赖具体领域和有界次优参数。

Conclusion: 概率化地交替进行启发式引导和最小f值扩展，能够有效缓解FOCAL准入延迟，尤其适用于f_min平台较长的搜索问题。该机制具有一定跨算法迁移性，但收益并非普遍存在，主要取决于确定性搜索的推进效率、问题结构及搜索界限。

Abstract: Bounded-suboptimal search seeks a solution within a factor $w$ of optimal while reducing search effort. Focal Search (FS) uses heuristic guidance within FOCAL, the frontier nodes eligible under the threshold $w f_{\min}$, but its deterministic policy may leave $f_{\min}$ unchanged for many expansions. We introduce Probabilistic Focal Search (PFS), which follows the FS guided choice with probability $p$ and expands a minimum-$f$ OPEN node with probability $1-p$. The latter branch encourages the lower bound to advance, enlarging FOCAL and admitting nodes that may lead to feasible solutions. By balancing guidance and lower-bound advancement, this mechanism can reduce time to a bounded solution when progress is limited by delayed FOCAL admission. As a secondary transfer experiment, we apply the same scheduler to Dynamic Potential Search, yielding Probabilistic Dynamic Potential Search (PDPS). We benchmark PFS against FS on N-Puzzle, Pancake Sorting, and the Traveling Salesperson Problem (TSP), and evaluate its anytime extension on the Generalized Covering TSP (GCTSP), using multiple $w$ and $p$ values. Across these benchmarks, the largest gains occur when long $f_{\min}$ plateaus delay useful FOCAL admissions; in such settings, the probabilistic factor may reduce node expansions by about 90\% or more (e.g., on N-Puzzle and TSP). For the anytime algorithm family, Anytime Probabilistic Focal Search (APFS) outperforms all tested algorithms in evaluating anytime methods on GCTSP. We also observe that the benefit is smaller when the deterministic search already advances efficiently (e.g., Pancake Sorting), indicating that the probabilistic factor is most useful when FOCAL admission is a search bottleneck. The PDPS transfer shows that the mechanism also transfers to potential guidance, although its common-success effects remain domain- and bound-dependent.

</details>


### [78] [Automating Quadratic Unconstrained Binary Optimization (QUBO) Formulation Generation from Natural Language](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10629)
*Niloy Kumar Mondal,Md Rizwan Parvez*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一个端到端多智能体框架，将自然语言问题自动转换为QUBO公式，并发布包含100个问题、覆盖12个应用领域的QUBOBench基准。框架在基准上的准确率达到68%，比直接单次调用基线高22个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: QUBO广泛适用于组合优化，并兼容量子、混合量子经典及量子启发式求解器。然而，从自然语言描述中准确识别二进制变量、目标函数、约束、惩罚项及惩罚权重，过程复杂且依赖领域知识，限制了QUBO建模的应用效率。

Method: 构建端到端多智能体系统，根据自然语言问题描述及结构化或非结构化测试用例自动生成QUBO formulation。系统引入迭代式自修复机制，以发现并修正生成结果中的建模错误。同时构建QUBOBench，收集100个组合优化问题，覆盖12个应用领域，来源包括同行评审文献、竞赛和经典NP难问题，并与直接单次调用基线进行比较。

Result: 框架在QUBOBench上的准确率为68%，较直接单次调用基线提升22个百分点。消融与进一步分析表明，迭代式自修复是提升系统性能的最关键组件。数据集与代码已公开。

Conclusion: 多智能体协作与迭代自修复能够有效提升自然语言到QUBO自动建模的准确性。QUBOBench为该任务提供了统一评测基准，但68%的准确率也表明复杂约束建模、惩罚权重选择和结果验证仍有进一步改进空间。

Abstract: Quadratic Unconstrained Binary Optimization (QUBO) is a central formulation for combinatorial optimization and has gained increasing attention due to its compatibility with quantum, hybrid quantum-classical, and quantum-inspired solvers. However, translating natural-language problem descriptions into correct QUBO formulations remains difficult, requiring the identification of binary variables, constraints, objective functions, penalty terms, and suitable penalty weights. This process is time-consuming and often demands substantial domain expertise. To address this challenge, we propose an end-to-end multi-agent framework that automatically generates QUBO formulations from natural-language problem descriptions, supported by structured or unstructured test cases. To evaluate its performance, We also introduce QUBOBench, a benchmark containing 100 combinatorial optimization problems across 12 application domains, curated from peer-reviewed literature, competitions, and canonical NP-hard problems. Experimental results show that our framework achieves 68% accuracy on QUBOBench, outperforming a direct single-call baseline by 22%. Further analysis identifies iterative self-repair as the most important component contributing to improved performance. The data and code are open-sourced at https://quitttcat.github.io/QuantumQUBOAgent.

</details>


### [79] [A Multi-Stage Rule-Chaining Framework for Compositional and Interpretable Cognitive Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10654)
*Deblina Kar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向ARC任务的多阶段规则链推理框架，结合确定性规则发现、模式组合和结构抽象三个求解器，通过渐进式回退与推理轨迹复用实现符号、结构和概念层面的组合推理。实验显示其在ARC-AGI及ARC-AGI-2任务上取得较高覆盖率与准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ARC旨在测试系统能否从少量示例中归纳抽象规则并迁移到新任务。现有方法通常在确定性变换、复杂模式组合或层次结构理解中的某一方面存在局限，且推理过程缺乏可解释性。本文因此尝试构建一个能够统一处理多类抽象关系、提高泛化能力并保留中间推理过程的架构。

Method: 框架由三个依次调用的求解器组成：第一阶段通过几何、颜色和对象分析发现原子变换规则；第二阶段利用块合并、重复和空间启发式重建输出模式；第三阶段识别网格中的层次化与嵌套结构。三个阶段采用渐进式回退层级，后续阶段可复用前序阶段产生的规则和推理轨迹，从而支持规则链组合、失败后的替代求解以及更强的可解释性。

Result: 系统在训练任务中解决995/1000个任务，在另一组评估任务中解决105/120个任务，并在ARC-AGI-2测试集上解决230/240个任务。230/240对应约95.8%的准确率，995/1000对应99.5%，105/120对应87.5%。摘要声称总体准确率超过95%，但未明确说明总体指标的计算方式、不同数据集是否加权以及各类别的具体表现。

Conclusion: 结果表明，将确定性符号规则、模式合成和层次结构抽象结合起来，可能有效提升ARC类任务中的组合泛化与可解释推理能力。该方法的主要贡献在于统一多层次推理并复用中间规则。不过，摘要缺少基线比较、独立测试协议、统计显著性、错误案例及对“无需任务特定调优”这一结论的充分证据，因此其泛化优势仍需通过更严格的对照实验验证。

Abstract: The Abstraction and Reasoning Corpus (ARC) benchmarks cognitive generalization, the ability to infer and apply abstract rules from limited examples. This paper presents a multi-stage rule-chaining framework that performs compositional reasoning across symbolic, structural, and conceptual levels. The framework integrates three complementary solvers:
  (1) a deterministic rule discovery module that induces atomic transformations through geometric, color, and object-based analysis;
  (2) a pattern-composition engine that reconstructs outputs via block merging, repetition, and spatial heuristics; and
  (3) a structural abstraction layer that infers hierarchical and nested relationships across grids.
  These solvers operate sequentially within a progressive fallback hierarchy, where each stage reuses prior reasoning traces to enhance interpretability and generalization. Training passed for 995 tasks out of 1000, further evaluated on 105 tasks out of 120 and solved 230 test tasks out of 240 ARC-AGI-2 tasks. The system achieved strong coverage across deterministic, compositional, and abstract categories, demonstrating an overall accuracy exceeding 95 percent. The proposed architecture bridges symbolic reasoning and pattern synthesis, providing interpretable insight into cognitive generalization. The results suggest that rule chaining and hierarchical composition can advance machine reasoning toward transparent, human-aligned abstraction without relying on task-specific tuning.

</details>


### [80] [Understanding LoRA Rank Trade-offs in Diffusion Model Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10656)
*Iman Khazrak,Narges Nejad,Mostafa M. Rezaee,Robert C. Green*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究扩散模型微调中LoRA秩的选择问题。基于CIFAR-10上的DDPM U-Net实验，结果表明中低秩设置具有更好的质量与计算成本平衡，其中秩4取得最佳FID 124.1380，秩8表现接近，为固定训练预算下的实用默认选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LoRA秩决定可训练参数规模、训练成本和模型适应能力。秩过低可能限制生成质量，秩过高则会增加运行时间与GPU显存消耗，因此需要在统一优化条件下系统评估不同秩的效果与效率。

Method: 研究在CIFAR-10上使用DDPM U-Net，比较LoRA秩2、4、8、16和32，并固定优化设置。采用可复现的本地文件夹pytorch-fid协议评估生成质量，同时记录FID、可训练参数量、训练运行时间和GPU显存。随后通过更长训练预算的DDPM实验（20个epoch，秩4/8/16）以及Tiny DiT实验（10个epoch，秩4/8/16）验证主要趋势。

Result: 在基础DDPM实验中，秩4取得最佳FID 124.1380，秩8的FID为124.2136，二者差异很小。更高秩虽然增加了适配参数、运行时间和显存开销，但没有带来相称的FID改善。扩展训练预算和更换为Tiny DiT骨干网络后的实验用于验证该趋势，摘要指出总体结论保持一致。

Conclusion: 在固定训练预算下，LoRA秩4至8能够提供较好的生成质量与计算效率平衡。提高秩通常会显著增加适配成本，但收益有限，因此小到中等秩可作为扩散模型微调的实践默认设置。由于摘要未给出扩展实验的完整数值、统计显著性或多次运行方差，跨架构结论仍需结合完整实验数据进一步确认。

Abstract: Selecting LoRA rank for diffusion fine-tuning requires balancing quality and compute cost. We present a controlled study on CIFAR-10 using a DDPM U-Net with ranks {2,4,8,16,32}, fixed optimization settings, and a reproducible local-folder pytorch-fid protocol. We report FID, trainable parameters, runtime, and GPU memory, then validate trends with extended-budget DDPM runs (20 epochs; ranks 4/8/16) and a Tiny DiT backbone (10 epochs; ranks 4/8/16). Results show moderate ranks are most efficient: rank 4 achieves the best DDPM FID (124.1380), rank 8 is close (124.2136), and higher ranks provide limited gains despite larger adaptation cost. These findings support small-to-moderate ranks as practical defaults under fixed training budgets.

</details>


### [81] [Quantifying the Memorization-to-Generalization Transition: Scaling Laws and Phase Structure in Grokking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10657)
*Anish Kataria*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文系统研究过拟合记忆向泛化转变（grokking）的发生时间，基于384组双隐藏层MLP模块算术实验，建立了超参数与泛化起始时间之间的幂律关系。结果显示，数据规模是最主要的影响因素，其作用显著强于模型宽度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究较多解释grokking为何发生，但对其在超参数空间中何时发生、哪些因素主导转变，缺乏定量刻画。本文旨在绘制记忆到泛化的边界，并建立可预测泛化起始时间的经验尺度关系。

Method: 在模块算术任务上测试384种双隐藏层MLP配置，系统改变隐藏层宽度H、数据规模D、学习率η和权重衰减λ，测量泛化起始时间T_grok。研究者拟合幂律模型T_grok∝H^-0.27 D^-2.04 η^-0.50 λ^-0.64，并比较包含交互项的模型，同时分析权重范数在转变过程中的变化及权重衰减导致的相变边界。

Result: 幂律模型的R²为0.732，加入交互项后提升至0.821。指数绝对值表明数据规模的影响最大（D^-2.04），其次为学习率（η^-0.50）和权重衰减（λ^-0.64），模型宽度影响最弱（H^-0.27）。数据量加倍可使泛化约提前4倍，而宽度加倍仅带来约1.2倍加速。当λ大于约1.0时出现明显的grokking与非grokking分界。权重范数在转变期间持续压缩，支持隐式正则化促使模型选择低复杂度解的解释。

Conclusion: grokking的发生时间主要由数据复杂度而非模型容量决定，且受到学习率和权重衰减的显著调节。本文提供了一个具有中等至较强解释力的经验预测框架，并表明权重衰减可能通过持续压缩权重、推动低复杂度解的形成来促成泛化。由于实验集中于模块算术和特定MLP架构，结论对其他任务、架构及优化器的可迁移性仍需进一步验证。

Abstract: Neural networks trained past memorization frequently undergo a delayed transition to generalization, a phenomenon known as grokking. Despite theoretical progress on \emph{why} this transition occurs, the quantitative structure of \emph{when} it occurs in hyperparameter space remains uncharacterized. We map the memorization-to-generalization boundary across 384 configurations of two-hidden-layer MLPs on modular arithmetic, fitting a power-law scaling relation for generalization onset time: $T_{\mathrm{grok}} \propto H^{-0.27}\, D^{-2.04}\, η^{-0.50}\, λ^{-0.64}$ ($R^2 = 0.732$; $0.821$ with interactions). The exponent hierarchy reveals that data complexity ($D^{-2.04}$) is the dominant driver of regime transition, not model capacity ($H^{-0.27}$): doubling data accelerates generalization by ${\sim}4\times$, while doubling width yields only ${\sim}1.2\times$. A sharp phase boundary at weight decay $λ\gtrsim 1.0$ separates grokking from non-grokking configurations, and weight norm trajectories show monotonic compression during the transition, consistent with implicit regularization selecting low-complexity solutions. These results provide a quantitative foundation for predicting and controlling regime transitions in overparameterized networks.

</details>

Abstract: We study how model post-training and test-time inference design affect natural-language proof generation for hard olympiad mathematics. Starting from Nemotron 3 Ultra, we train two specialist checkpoints using supervised fine-tuning and reinforcement learning, and evaluate checkpoint choice, verification, and refinement. Based on these findings, we present an open-model test-time-compute pipeline. The system operates entirely in natural language, with no formal prover, external tools, or internet access. Three Nemotron 3 Ultra checkpoints - the general-availability model and two post-trained specialists - power an iterative search that generates, verifies, and refines candidate proofs; a separate high-compute stage then selects each final submission. The system scored 30 out of 42 points at IMO 2026, reaching the gold-medal threshold. We release the two post-trained checkpoints as well as the training data, the training and inference code, the submitted solutions, and Nemotron-IMO-Bench, a new benchmark of 200 novel olympiad-level problems.

</details>


### [83] [Finishing the Task Is Not Enough: Evaluating Agent Resilience and Considerate Participation under Accumulating Challenge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10724)
*Yuanchen Bai,Zijian Ding,Angelique Taylor*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究生成式AI代理在持续、受扰动的协作工作流中的运营韧性与体贴式参与，发现挑战累积会促使代理更多依赖人类、扩大协调范围，但其显性语言与结构化状态报告存在不一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单次任务成功不足以支撑生成式AI代理的长期部署。代理还必须在反复交互、环境变化、任务受阻及多人协作依赖下保持有效，并能透明沟通限制、尊重角色边界和关注受影响人员。现有研究较少考察这些能力在持续累积挑战下的变化。

Method: 构建包含两种生成式AI模型、12项利益相关者任务和120条模拟医疗工作流轨迹的实验，设置轻度、中度和重度挑战。通过比较文本行动计划、提示式内部评估，以及结构化工作负荷和情绪状态报告，分析挑战累积时代理行为与自我报告状态的变化。

Result: 在运营韧性方面，代理由自主恢复逐渐转向更依赖人类；结构化报告显示工作负荷和负性情绪上升，但文本回复很少直接表达压力。在体贴式参与方面，代理从围绕任务的适应扩展到任务重构、关注他人、调整角色边界和更广泛协调；行动表现与内部评估呈现不同模式。研究进一步归纳出围绕坚持、注意、角色边界、状态披露和升级处理的五类部署困境。

Conclusion: 运营韧性与体贴式参与应作为评估长期部署AI代理的互补维度，并需由利益相关者明确规范相关权衡。研究结果为代理学习机制、情境化评估、状态披露和具身适应设计提供了方向。

Abstract: Sustained deployment of generative AI agents requires more than isolated task success. Agents must remain useful across repeated interactions, changing conditions, and dependencies on people within shared workflows, especially as technical, human, and operational disruptions accumulate over time. We propose operational resilience and considerate participation as two complementary aspects of evaluating such agents: the former captures how agents recover from blocked work while preserving progress and communicating their limits, and the latter captures how their adaptation accounts for affected people, role boundaries, and the surrounding workflow. Yet both remain underexplored under accumulating challenge. We study 120 simulated healthcare trajectories across two generative AI models and twelve stakeholder-derived tasks under light, medium, and heavy challenge. We compare textual action plans, prompted internal assessments, and quantitative structured workload and affect reports to examine how agent behavior and reported state change as challenge accumulates. Regarding operational resilience, agents shift from self-directed recovery toward greater human dependence, while reporting increasing workload and negative affect in structured reports but seldom expressing strain in textual responses. Regarding considerate participation, agents broaden from task-focused adaptation toward task reframing, attention to others, role-boundary adjustment, and wider coordination, with distinct patterns across actions and internal assessments. From these findings, we derive five deployment dilemmas involving persistence, attention, role boundaries, state disclosure, and escalation that require stakeholder specification, further informing technical implications for learning, situated evaluation, and embodied adaptation.

</details>


### [84] [Towards a Deterministic Math Solver for Clinical Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10728)
*Felipe Ocampo Osorio,Sebastián Andrés Cajas Ordoñez,Maximin Lange,Rafi Al Attrach,Sahil Kapadia,Zakaria Laouabdia Sellami,Angelo Antonio Talio,Leo Anthony Celi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究评估了让大语言模型生成病例专用Python代码、再由受限执行器确定性计算临床公式的方法。该方法对7B模型优势不稳定，但显著提升了32B模型的准确率；手写计算器库在覆盖范围内准确，却因大量弃答导致总体表现有限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床计算器中的单次数值错误可能改变临床建议，而大语言模型直接算术不可靠。逐一手写并验证计算器成本较高，因此研究探索将计算任务交给确定性执行器、让模型主要负责调用和组织计算。

Method: 在MedCalc-Bench Verified上评估，数据包含1100个病例和55种计算器。比较Program-Solve接口、模型直接算术以及支持22种计算器的手写库，使用Qwen2.5-7B和Qwen2.5-32B-AWQ。所有方法均提供公式、金标准变量并读取完整病例记录；同时依据当前临床指南审计公式，发现55种计算器中有16种存在版本、适用场景或系数问题。

Result: 7B模型中，Program-Solve准确率为75.31%，直接算术为72.02%，提升3.29个百分点，但95%计算器聚类区间为[-3.49, 10.38]，未显示可靠优势。32B模型中，Program-Solve为90.53%，直接算术为83.47%，提升7.05个百分点，95%区间为[0.47, 14.60]，明确高于零。手写库在其支持的440个病例上达到100%准确率，但因其他病例弃答，总体准确率仅40.0%。

Conclusion: 受限执行器能够提升部分更大规模开放权重模型的临床计算表现，但收益依赖模型能力，并非普遍可靠。该方法不能替代经过验证的公式，也不能解决变量提取不准确的问题；公式审计、变量识别和适当调用仍是系统可靠性的关键。

Abstract: Large language models are unreliable at arithmetic, which is a problem for clinical calculators where a single numerical error changes the recommendation. The standard response is to hardcode each calculator as a validated function, one at a time. We test an alternative: the model does not calculate. Instead, it writes case-specific Python that a restricted local executor runs as a deterministic solver, and the model's task reduces to deciding how to use it. We evaluate this Program-Solve interface on MedCalc-Bench Verified (1,100 cases, 55 calculators) against direct model arithmetic and a hand-written 22-calculator library, using Qwen2.5-7B and Qwen2.5-32B-AWQ, after auditing the benchmark's formulas against current clinical guidelines and flagging 16 of 55 with version, use or coefficient concerns. With formulas and gold variables supplied and both routes reading the whole note, handing off to the solver is not a reliable advantage at 7B (75.31% against 72.02%, a paired +3.29 points with a 95% calculator-cluster interval of [-3.49, 10.38]) but is one at 32B (90.53% against 83.47%, +7.05 [0.47, 14.60], clear of zero). The hand-written library is exact on its 440 supported cases but abstains elsewhere (40.0% overall). Adding an executor thus helps some open-weight models more than others even under matched formula, variable and note access, and is not a substitute for verified formulas or reliable variable extraction either way.

</details>


### [85] [Extending SMT Solving with Non-Ground Clause Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11509)
*Yasmine Briefs,Christoph Weidenbach*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种结合量词实例化、CDCL(T)推理与非地面冲突分析的统一演算，使求解器能够从地面冲突中学习更一般、通常更短且可保持非冗余的非地面子句。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有非地面SMT求解主要依赖量词实例化：求解器将公式地面化后进行CDCL(T)推理，但冲突分析只能学习地面子句，无法利用原始非地面子句所蕴含的更紧凑证明，可能导致指数级的证明长度开销。因此，需要在保持地面推理可操作性的同时引入非地面推理能力。

Method: 构建一个统一演算，包含地面实例化、CDCL(T)风格规则和非地面冲突分析。求解过程仍基于地面实例，但冲突分析中的归结步骤回溯到对应的原始非地面子句，从而生成更一般的学习子句；通过适当策略保证学习子句通常具有非冗余性。此外，将按时间顺序回溯机制纳入SMT求解，并在统一框架下刻画多种实例化过程与非地面子句学习。

Result: 理论上证明该演算能够模拟CDCL、SCL(FOL)、SCL(T)以及Resolution，说明其覆盖现有多类命题、SMT和一阶逻辑推理机制。该方法还能产生比纯地面冲突分析更一般的学习子句，并有潜力获得指数级更短的证明。

Conclusion: 非地面冲突分析可以有效弥合量词实例化与高层逻辑推理之间的差距，在保留CDCL(T)式地面求解框架的同时提升证明紧凑性和表达能力。所提出的演算为CDCL(T)、实例化型求解器和非地面子句学习提供了统一且具有完备模拟能力的理论基础。

Abstract: Quantifier instantiation is currently the main approach to non-ground SMT solving: solvers generate ground instances and solve the resulting ground SMT problems with CDCL(T)-style reasoning. When a conflict is found, conflict analysis learns only a ground clause, even though the conflict comes from instances of non-ground clauses. Yet non-ground reasoning can give exponentially shorter proofs than purely ground reasoning. We propose a calculus that consists of ground instantiations, CDCL(T)-style rules, and non-ground conflict analysis. The solver reasons on ground instances, but the resolution steps of conflict analysis are performed on their original non-ground clauses. This produces learned clauses that are typically more general than the ground conflict. With a suitable strategy, the learned clauses are even non-redundant. We also show how chronological backtracking can be included in SMT solving. Our calculus gives a common setting for CDCL(T)-style SMT solving, a range of instantiation-based procedures, and non-ground clause learning, and we prove that it simulates CDCL, SCL(FOL), SCL(T), and even Resolution.

</details>

*Vinay Samuel,Varun Ursekar,Vijay S. Kalmath,Apaar Shanker,Veronica Chatrath,Yuan Xue*

Main category: cs.AI

Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Abstract: Before an LLM agent tackles tasks in a new environment, it can inspect available corpora and tools and construct reusable resources such as indices, scripts, or procedural guidance. Most automated adaptation methods, however, rely on task examples, trajectories, or evaluation feedback to decide what to build. Existing task-agnostic approaches avoid this supervision but commit in advance to a preparation strategy for a particular type of environment. We study a more open-ended setting: can an agent study an unfamiliar environment without a syllabus, i.e. before test time and without knowledge of the downstream task distribution, and choose how to prepare it? We formalize task-agnostic environment preprocessing, in which a studying system explores an environment under a budget and produces artifacts for a frozen solver. We compare unaided and archive-equipped meta-agents with fixed synthetic-practice and corpus-processing methods across six heterogeneous benchmarks. A meta-agent variant achieves the highest Avg@3 reward on five benchmarks, while fixed corpus processing remains best on the largest corpus benchmark. Larger study budgets do not reliably improve downstream reward. Nevertheless, studied artifacts reduce the test-time sampling needed to reach a given score, demonstrating how reusable preparation can shift computation from repeated test-time attempts to a pre-task study phase.

</details>


### [87] [When Validation Stops Learning: Auditing Update Admission for Continual Embodied Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10873)
*Qinzhen Ma,Ruihai Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种更新准入审计协议，用于在持续学习中同时控制旧任务性能错误并保留有价值的学习机会。实验表明，配对二项式检验比基于区间的置信门控更能避免过度拒绝更新，但无条件回放在闭环学习中仍可能取得更好效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 独立评估虽然能够拒绝有害的策略更新，却可能阻断有益的持续学习。现有基于范围的置信门控在有限交互预算下无法充分证明旧任务行为未发生变化，因此需要同时衡量错误控制、学习机会保留以及交互成本。

Method: 研究首先构造并分析范围式置信门控的失效情形；随后采用标准配对二项式方法，在样本结果不一致较少时降低认证负担。方法还包括经认证的历史参考策略提升机制，以及按轮次统计的学习机会遗漏指标。实验使用单步推送诊断、32个随机种子和每阶段2000回合的预算，并通过学习动力学压力测试区分模型偏差与反馈选择误差。

Result: 在构造的单步推送诊断中，新鲜配对检验准入了常见更新流的31.6%，而范围式门控的准入率为0。在闭环运行中，无条件回放的学习效果反而更好，说明严格的准入控制可能牺牲有效学习机会。独立的学习动力学压力测试进一步表明，模型偏差与反馈选择错误是可区分的因素。

Conclusion: 本文提供了一个结合解析证明与合成实验的更新准入审计框架，强调应在明确交互预算下联合评估安全性与持续学习能力。结果支持配对检验作为降低过度拒绝的实用工具，但尚不足以证明其在真实机器人和视觉语言动作系统中的有效性；物理机器人及VLA验证仍待开展。

Abstract: Independent evaluation can reject harmful policy updates yet also prevent useful continual learning. We argue that update admission must be assessed through both error control and retained learning opportunities at a stated interaction budget. We identify a concrete failure: a range-based confidence gate cannot certify unchanged old-task behavior within otherwise substantial budgets. A standard paired-binomial construction reduces this burden when outcome disagreements are rare. We also specify certified historical-reference promotion and a round-level missed-opportunity metric. In a constructed one-step pushing diagnostic with 32 seeds, fresh paired checks admit 31.6% of a common update stream at 2,000 episodes per stage, versus zero for the range-based gate; unconditional replay nevertheless learns better in closed-loop runs. A separate learned-dynamics stress test distinguishes model bias from feedback-selection error. The contribution is an admission-audit protocol with analytical and synthetic evidence; physical-robot and VLA validation remain open.

</details>


### [88] [Decoupling Readiness from Release for Tail-Aware Scheduling of Agentic LLM Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10964)
*Bochao Feng,Jianjiang Li,Haojie Wang,Lin Qiao,Yinghui Li,Yukun Yan,Jidong Zhai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出一种面向智能体大模型工作流的周转调度方法，通过控制就绪模型轮次的释放顺序与未完成已释放工作量，降低资源竞争下工作流完成时间的长尾延迟；在高负载场景下，P95工作流流动时间最高可获得3.50倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体工作流由模型推理与工具调用交替组成，端到端时延既受单次推理速度影响，也取决于就绪轮次何时提交。传统的即时释放策略在竞争条件下会累积大量已释放但尚未完成的任务；这些任务一旦进入底层队列，工作流层便难以重新排序，导致尾部请求等待时间显著增加。因此，需要在维持吞吐的同时显式管理尾部时延风险。

Method: 方法以均值-条件风险价值（mean-CVaR）为目标，刻画未完成工作流完成时间分布中不断变化的尾部风险。调度器联合决定两个变量：其一，多个就绪轮次中应优先释放哪个轮次；其二，系统中允许保留多少已释放但未完成的工作。轮次优先级结合在线估计的计算工作量；释放预算则依据观测到的队列压力动态调整，从而在负载较轻时接近即时释放，在拥塞时抑制过度提交。

Result: 实验采用来自软件工程任务的真实智能体执行轨迹，覆盖多种大模型及不同工作流到达率。结果表明，在轻载条件下，该方法与即时释放策略性能相当；在资源竞争明显时，能够显著降低工作流流动时间的P95，并实现最高3.50倍的加速。

Conclusion: 该工作说明，智能体大模型系统的性能优化不应仅关注推理吞吐，还应在工作流层管理任务释放时机。以CVaR为导向的风险感知调度及自适应在途工作预算，能够有效减少拥塞下的尾部时延，并保持轻载场景中的性能。

Abstract: Agentic LLM workflows consist of sequences of model turns interleaved with tool interactions, so their end-to-end completion time depends not only on inference speed but also on when ready turns are released. Most runtimes release each turn immediately upon readiness. Under contention, this eager release policy can accumulate released but unfinished work; once submitted, those turns can no longer be reordered by the workflow-level policy, increasing tail latency. We present a tail-risk-aware turn release scheduling method that jointly decides which ready turn to release next and how much released but unfinished work to maintain. The method uses a mean--Conditional Value-at-Risk (CVaR) objective to capture the evolving tail risk of unfinished workflows, incorporates online estimates of turn work when prioritizing ready turns, and adapts the released work budget to observed queue pressure. We evaluate the method using real agent execution traces from software engineering tasks across multiple LLMs and workflow arrival rates. The method performs comparably to eager release under light load and substantially reduces the P95 of workflow flow time under contention, achieving up to a \(3.50\times\) speedup.

</details>


### [89] [Demystifying the Privacy-Utility Trade-off in LLM Interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10992)
*Zhenhua Liu,Zhanxu Xie,Junjie Yu,Tong Zhu,Lijun Li,Wenliang Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文系统分析大语言模型上下文 sanitization 对隐私与任务效用的影响，提出由用户意图驱动的本地保护框架 Veilmind-4B，通过动态提取、脱敏与恢复，在低信息泄露的同时提升下游响应效用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 日常使用大语言模型需要提供包含敏感信息的丰富上下文，而现有基于静态且与上下文无关规则的隐私保护方法通常造成明显的效用损失。论文旨在解释脱敏影响任务性能的具体机制，并改善隐私与效用之间的权衡。

Method: 论文通过系统分析提出三种机制：一是上下文依赖效用，根据用户意图判断信息是关键约束还是可忽略噪声，从而决定是否脱敏；二是策略性适配，根据任务更依赖事实准确性还是结构连贯性，在删除与替换之间进行选择；三是组合交互，建模不同属性之间的协同依赖和冗余冲突，以扩大保护范围。在此基础上，作者使用轻量模型 Veilmind-4B 构建意图驱动的本地保护流程，包括动态信息提取、脱敏和结果恢复。

Result: 摘要声称该方法能够达到较低的信息泄露水平，并且相比现有面向隐私的基线方法保留更高的响应效用，使隐私与效用的平衡更接近 Pareto 前沿。但摘要未提供具体数据集、评价指标、泄露率、效用提升幅度或统计显著性。

Conclusion: 论文的核心贡献是将脱敏从静态规则问题转化为由任务意图和属性关系共同决定的动态决策问题，并据此提出 Veilmind-4B 框架。其结论具有合理的机制解释和方法创新，但仅凭摘要无法验证实际优势，仍需重点检查实验设置、隐私威胁模型、基线公平性、恢复阶段的信息泄露风险以及不同任务和领域上的泛化能力。

Abstract: The integration of Large Language Models into daily tasks relies on context-rich instructions, inevitably exposing sensitive user information. Current privacy-preserving methods typically employ context-agnostic static rules, causing severe utility degradation. However, the specific mechanisms governing how sanitization impacts downstream performance remain largely underexplored. To address this, we conduct a systematic analysis to deconstruct the privacy-utility trade-off, uncovering three underlying mechanisms: (1) Context-Dependent Utility, which first establishes when to sanitize by revealing that data value shifts from critical constraints to dispensable noise based on user intent; (2) Strategic Adaptation, which subsequently determines how to sanitize by dictating that the choice between removal and replacement depends on the task's reliance on factual integrity versus structural coherence; and (3) Combinatorial Interplay, which finally extends the protection scope by demonstrating that attributes form a semantic web of synergistic dependencies or antagonistic redundancies. Guided by these insights, we introduce an intent-driven local protection framework. By distilling a lightweight model Veilmind-4B to drive a dynamic extraction-sanitization-restoration pipeline, our approach reaches a low-leakage privacy point while preserving substantially higher response utility than existing privacy-oriented baselines, advancing the privacy-utility trade-off toward the Pareto frontier.

</details>


### [90] [Defining AI Agents: A Compendium of Criteria, Metrics, and Benchmarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11018)
*Mia Lassiter,Brinnae Bent*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文综述人工智能智能体评估研究，围绕环境交互、学习与适应、自主性、目标导向行为和时间连贯性五个维度，系统整理相关概念、指标、基准和评估框架，并构建公开资源 Agent Compendium。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: “智能体”缺乏统一定义，导致不同研究之间难以进行有效评估、比较与复现，因此需要建立结构化、可共享的评估框架。

Method: 采用综述方法，按五个智能体性维度梳理既有研究中的能力定义与概念化方式，汇总相应的测量指标、基准测试和评估框架；同时将整理出的评估方法组织并扩展为公开数字资源 Agent Compendium。

Result: 研究归纳了智能体评估领域的主要方法与现状，识别出较为成熟的评估路径以及评估不足、不一致和缺乏统一标准的领域，并形成了可供查询和扩展的评估资源。

Conclusion: 该综述与 Agent Compendium 为不同人工智能系统的能力评估和比较提供了共同结构，有助于提高研究可复现性、促进清晰交流，并推动对人工智能智能体的系统性研究。

Abstract: The term agent in artificial intelligence lacks a standard definition, complicating the evaluation, comparison, and reproducibility of AI agent research. We address this ambiguity through a survey organized around five dimensions of agenticness: environmental interaction, learning and adaptation, autonomy, goal-directed behavior, and temporal coherence. For each dimension, we examine how the underlying capability has been conceptualized across prior work and synthesize the metrics, benchmarks, and evaluation frameworks used to assess it. This review provides a structured account of the current landscape of agent evaluation, highlighting both established approaches and areas where evaluation remains limited or inconsistent. We additionally introduce the Agent Compendium, a public-facing digital resource that organizes and extends the evaluation methods identified through this review. Together, the survey and compendium provide a common structure for evaluating and comparing agent capabilities across AI systems, supporting more reproducible research, clearer communication, and more systematic study of artificial agents.

</details>


### [91] [The Agent Incident Registry: Toward Preventing Repeated AI Agent Failures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11030)
*Divyanshu Kumar,Rohith HN,Nitin Aravind Birur,Sahil Agarwal,Prashanth Harshangi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 Agent Incident Registry（AIR），一个带有来源链接、稳定标识符和缺失感知标签的智能体事件目录，用于系统比较公开事件与智能体安全评估。结果显示，已记录的生成式系统事件中，实际造成危害的比例有限且受样本构成影响，不能据此估计部署风险、失败率或控制措施有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有通用事件数据库通常缺少比较智能体公开失败事件与安全评估所需的机制、因果角色、披露类型和结果信息。研究旨在建立一个可追溯、可复核的数据基础，以支持基于来源的事件检索和评估范围审计。

Method: 研究构建 AIR，收录从指定起始时间至指定终止时间披露的智能体相关事件。每条记录包含证据来源、稳定标识符，以及因果角色、披露类别、作用机制和结果等缺失感知标签。初步整理后，第二名人工审阅者复核全部记录及既有标签的完整性和正确性。研究还将 InjecAgent 的案例映射到 AIR 的事件表面，以开展部署类比审计，并将其与 AIR 中无对手参与的安全失败进行比较。

Result: 在生成式系统且智能体实际执行行动的记录中，Rprimary 条涉及实际危害，占 Pprimary%，但具体数值仍以占位符表示。实际结果主要集中于真实环境事件和安全失败记录；负责任披露与研究演示几乎全部属于演示性结果，因此总体比例反映的是数据收集构成，而非部署风险。InjecAgent 的 NInjecAgentCases 个案例覆盖 AIR 十二个表面中的三个，且全部由攻击者触发；AIR 另包含 Nsafety 个无对手参与的安全失败事件。全部 N 条记录均经过第二名人工审阅者检查。

Conclusion: AIR 的主要贡献是提供来源可核验、标签结构化且支持缺失值表达的智能体事件登记框架，可用于案例检索和安全评估覆盖范围审计。研究明确限制了数据的推断边界：事件数量和危害比例不能直接用于估计真实部署中的失败率、风险水平或控制措施效果。摘要中的 N、Yfirst、Ylast、Nprimary、Rprimary、Pprimary、NInjecAgentCases 和 Nsafety 尚未替换为实际数值或时间范围，正式发表前需要补全。

Abstract: AI agents increasingly act through tools and delegated authority, but general incident repositories rarely capture the mechanisms needed to compare public failures with agent-security evaluations. We present the Agent Incident Registry (AIR), a source-linked catalog containing \N{} records of agent-related events disclosed from \Yfirst{} through \Ylast{}. Each record includes supporting evidence, a stable identifier, and missingness-aware labels for causal role, disclosure class, mechanism, and outcome. Among the \Nprimary{} generative-system records in which the agent acted, \Rprimary{} involved realized harm (\Pprimary\%). Realized outcomes concentrate in in-the-wild and safety-failure records, while responsible disclosures and research demonstrations are overwhelmingly demonstrated; the aggregate share therefore characterizes collection composition rather than deployment risk. After initial curation, a second human reviewer checked all \N{} records and their existing labels for completeness and correctness. In a deployment-analogue audit, InjecAgent's \NInjecAgentCases{} cases occupy three of AIR's twelve surfaces and are all attacker-triggered, whereas AIR contains \Nsafety{} no-adversary safety failures. AIR supports source-grounded case retrieval and evaluation-scope auditing, not failure-rate or control-efficacy estimation.

</details>


### [92] [Grounding Agent Memory: Environment-Probing Curation for Enterprise Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11060)
*Susheel Suresh,Hazel Mak,Sahil Bhatnagar,Chhaya Methani,Alejandro Gutierrez Munoz*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出环境探测式记忆整理方法，使异步记忆整理代理能够以最小权限、只读方式验证和更新候选记忆，在不重新训练模型或改变生产系统核心组件的情况下提升长程代理性能与成本效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仅依据已完成轨迹整理记忆，容易保留错误、过度泛化不完整证据或沉淀过时知识。需要一种部署兼容、可审计且不增加任务代理接口复杂度的记忆验证机制。

Method: 在基于 GitHub Copilot SDK 的生产式测试环境中，为现有异步整理代理提供最小权限的只读环境工具，使其能够检查记忆适用范围、验证事实并刷新候选记忆。对比无状态执行、完整上下文学习、GHCP + Mem 和 GHCP + Mem（环境探测）四种设置，并在 CLBench 数据库探索任务及 90 个改编的 APEX 管理咨询任务上评估。

Result: 在 CLBench 上，环境探测将通过率从 39% 提升至 73%，按通过率折扣的奖励从 8.60 提升至 22.60；每题查询次数从 8.8 降至 4.7，任务代理成本从 3.38 美元降至 1.68 美元。在六个 APEX 环境中，18 组记忆方法与基线的平均奖励比较全部为正，任务代理工具调用减少 16%—75%；环境探测在五个环境中取得最高的任务代理单位成本奖励增益。在 Sonnet 4.6 和 Opus 4.7 上，其平均奖励均高于 GHCP + Mem，且未出现模式漂移。

Conclusion: 环境探测将记忆整理从基于轨迹的被动归纳转变为结合环境验证的可审计过程，能够减少错误和过时记忆带来的影响，同时提高任务成功率、降低工具调用与成本，并保持紧凑稳定的任务时接口。

Abstract: Persistent memory is entering production-oriented agent platforms to help long-horizon agents accumulate experience across sessions. Yet a post-task curator agent restricted to completed trajectories can preserve errors, overgeneralize partial evidence, or retain stale knowledge. We introduce environment-probing curation, a deployment-compatible extension that gives an existing asynchronous curator agent least-privilege, read-only world tools to check, scope, and refresh candidate memories. It requires no model retraining and leaves the task agent, retriever, memory representation, and production write authority unchanged. In a production-like GitHub Copilot (GHCP) harness built on its SDK, we compare stateless execution, full in-context learning, GHCP + Mem, and GHCP + Mem (w/ Env Probing) on CLBench database exploration and 90 adapted APEX management-consulting tasks. On CLBench, probing raises pass rate from 39% to 73% and pass-discounted reward from 8.60 to 22.60 while reducing queries from 8.8 to 4.7 per question and task-agent cost from \$3.38 to \$1.68. Across six APEX worlds, all 18 memory-versus-baseline mean reward comparisons are positive and task-agent tool calls fall by 16--75%; probing gives the best task-agent reward gain per dollar in five worlds. Probing also attains higher mean reward than GHCP + Mem on both Sonnet 4.6 and Opus 4.7 without schema drift. Environment probing therefore turns existing agent-memory curation into an environment-informed, auditable process while preserving a compact task-time interface.

</details>


### [93] [Fork Where the Model Changes Its Mind: Belief-Shift Branching for Tree-Structured Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11061)
*Bin Lei,Yu Li,Prafulla Kumar Choubey,Jiaxin Zhang,Becky Xiangyu Peng,Qinyuan Ye,Kartik Narayan,Caiwen Ding,Silvio Savarese,Chien-Sheng Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出“信念转移分支”（belief-shift branching），通过识别模型对答案信念变化最大的步骤来放置树状 rollout 的分叉点，从而以较少采样成本获得更有效的步级信用信号。在多模型、多基准实验中，该方法整体优于结构式、熵式和 LLM 评审式基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 树状 RLVR 依靠同一中间状态下不同后续结果的差异来估计步骤价值，但每条链可用的分叉数量有限。若分叉发生在结果已基本确定的位置，兄弟分支结果高度一致，几乎无法提供有效信用信号。因此，分叉位置是决定步级强化学习收益的关键因素。

Method: 将分叉点定位形式化为寻找链上价值曲线的“枢轴”位置，即期望结果发生明显转折的位置。方法在候选边界读取模型的答案信念，并在连续信念差异最大的位置之前进行分叉。提出三种无需步级监督的实现：黑盒探测、基于 logit-lens 的深度信念剖面，以及离线拟合的激活方向。该信号仅用于分叉定位；在线探测的额外计算成本在数学任务约为步计算量的 1%，代码任务低于 5%。

Result: 在与蒙特卡洛价值曲线的验证中，信念转移信号在 8 个模型×基准测试面板中均排名第一，超过熵、结构规则和 LLM 评审基线。RL 实验覆盖 3 个模型系列和 2 个领域，信念转移分支在所有数学综合指标上领先；在 OLMo-3-7B 上，相比最强基线综合提升 2.6 个百分点，在 AIME 2026 上提升 2.9 个百分点；在代码任务中，所有 OLMo 代码指标均获胜，并在 LiveCodeBench-medium 上提升 6.5 个百分点。

Conclusion: 分叉应依据模型答案信念的关键变化点，而非固定结构或局部熵进行放置。信念转移分支在低分叉预算下能更有效地产生步级信用信号，并以较低额外开销稳定提升 RLVR 性能，说明价值曲线枢轴是树状 rollout 分支选择的有效依据。

Abstract: Tree-structured rollouts give critic-free reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) step-level credit: fork a chain at an intermediate point, and sibling outcome differences estimate step value. Each fork adds sampling cost, so realistic budgets typically allow only a few forks per chain. A fork placed where the outcome is already largely settled yields siblings that mostly agree and provide almost no credit signal; hence, for a given tree size, where forks are placed largely determines how much step-level RL can gain. Most existing mainstream methods place forks by structure, such as fixed lengths, midpoints, and delimiters, or by next-token entropy. We formalize fork placement as locating the \emph{pivots} of the chain's value curve, where the expected outcome turns. We propose \emph{belief-shift branching}: read the model's answer belief at candidate boundaries and fork just before the step where consecutive beliefs diverge most. Three instantiations, none needing step-level supervision, span access levels: a black-box probe, a logit-lens depth profile, and a learned activation direction, which is fit offline and therefore used only in the validation before RL training. The signal only \emph{places} forks, and the probe costs about $1\%$ of step compute on mathematics and under $5\%$ on code when it runs inside the rollout engine. In that validation, against Monte-Carlo value curves, a belief-shift signal ranks first in each of the eight model$\times$benchmark panels, ahead of entropy, structural, and LLM-judge baselines. In RL across three model families and two domains, belief-shift forking leads every mathematics aggregate, on OLMo-3-7B by $+2.6$ aggregate and $+2.9$ on AIME 2026 over the strongest baseline, and sweeps every OLMo code column, by $+6.5$ on LiveCodeBench-medium.

</details>


### [94] [MOSAIC: Query-Aware Exploration Policy Adaptation for GraphRAG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11065)
*EunKyeong Lee,Kyeong-Jin Oh,Jinwon Kim,Hye Woo Lee,Minsang Song,Hyeongjun Jang,Junyoung Youn*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Mosaic提出一种无需训练的GraphRAG查询自适应检索框架，通过根据问题类型动态控制种子选择、图遍历、停止和证据筛选，显著提升答案正确性、证据召回与检索效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GraphRAG系统通常对不同查询采用近似共享的探索策略，无法适配直接事实、跨目标比较和多跳中介推理等不同证据需求，导致检索范围、深度与证据规模难以同时优化。

Method: Mosaic将GraphRAG检索建模为逐查询控制问题。LLM分析器把查询所需的证据结构转换为有界策略，控制种子选择、图遍历方向与深度、停止条件及证据选择；语料图、索引、评分函数、证据 grounding 流程和答案生成器保持共享。该方法无需针对具体基准训练检索器。

Result: 在GraphRAG-Bench上，Mosaic的查询加权答案正确性在Medical和Novel上分别达到76.97和64.33，较此前最佳整体结果分别提升5.13和4.43个百分点。Medical上的证据召回率为95.1，上下文相关性为86.1。在相同图和生成器的控制实验中，Mosaic较最强固定策略提升9.96个百分点；相较Fixed Wide，路径评估量减少81.9%，保留的证据项减少47.2%。在HotpotQA、MuSiQue和2WikiMultiHopQA上的迁移实验表明，该策略接口具有跨基准适用性。

Conclusion: 结果表明，GraphRAG的最优检索范围和深度取决于查询的证据结构，固定窄、中或宽策略并不存在普适优势。通过LLM驱动的查询级策略控制，Mosaic在保持共享系统组件和免训练的同时，实现了更高的答案质量、更好的证据覆盖和更低的检索成本。

Abstract: Graph Retrieval-Augmented Generation (GraphRAG) can connect evidence distributed across a corpus graph, but most systems use largely shared exploration procedures across queries. This creates a structural mismatch: direct facts may need compact local neighborhoods, comparisons need balanced coverage of multiple targets, and mediated questions may require deeper paths through weakly related connectors. We present Mosaic, a training-free framework that formulates GraphRAG retrieval as a per-query control problem. An LLM analyzer converts query-specific evidence requirements into a bounded policy over seed selection, graph traversal, stopping, and evidence selection, while the corpus graph, indexes, scoring functions, grounding procedure, and answer generator remain shared.
  On GraphRAG-Bench, Mosaic achieves query-weighted Answer Correctness of 76.97 on Medical and 64.33 on Novel, improving over the strongest previously reported overall results by 5.13 and 4.43 points. On Medical, it reaches 95.1 Evidence Recall and 86.1 Context Relevancy. Controlled comparisons on an identical graph and generator show that no fixed narrow, medium, or wide policy is consistently optimal; Mosaic improves by 9.96 points over the strongest canonical fixed policy. Relative to Fixed Wide, it evaluates 81.9% fewer paths and retains 47.2% fewer evidence items. Transfer experiments on HotpotQA, MuSiQue, and 2WikiMultiHopQA further show that the policy interface can be applied without benchmark-specific retriever training.

</details>


### [95] [Benchmark Radar: A Living Database and Search Engine for AI Benchmarks and Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11115)
*Koutian Wu,Junjie Zhou,Ergan Shang,Jiayu Wang,Pengqian Han,Junkai Wang,Wanghan Xu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Benchmark Radar 是一个持续更新的人工智能基准检索与发现系统，整合论文、代码库、数据集、模型报告中的基准信息及分数历史，支持基准选择、证据核查和趋势分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究人员和模型开发者需要高效发现相关评测基准，定位其数据集与代码，并理解分数背后的评测设置和证据来源。现有信息分散，且不同基准之间的分数可比性和使用情况缺乏系统整理。

Method: 系统通过13个直接连接器和24个一手研究与工程信息源进行每日发现，收集基准论文、代码库、数据集和版本发布信息；构建可搜索的基准目录，关联模型卡片与技术报告中的提及，保留来源身份、引用关系和分数历史，并提供目录审计、检索接口、命令行工具及可复现分析。

Result: 目录包含来自4个基准目录的1,283条来源记录，以及覆盖790条记录的12,916个数值观测。系统支持基准排行榜、分数与实际使用量的帕累托前沿、饱和度与趋势分析、每日信息流、证据下载和离线查询。案例研究展示了如何完成先验工作检索并核查基准证据。

Conclusion: Benchmark Radar 将分散的人工智能评测资源整合为可持续更新、可检索且可审计的基础设施，有助于设计新评测和评估基准采用趋势；但其分析也强调，跨基准分数比较受到评测设置、使用程度和证据完整性的限制。

Abstract: Benchmark researchers and developers of large language models (LLMs) and other AI systems need to find relevant evaluations, locate their benchmark datasets and code, and understand the settings behind reported scores. We present Benchmark Radar, a living database and search engine for retrieval and discovery of AI benchmarks, covering LLM evaluation, agentic and tool-use benchmarks, coding, reasoning, safety, and domain-specific evaluations. The system combines daily discovery of benchmark papers, repositories, datasets, and releases with a searchable benchmark catalog, mentions in model cards and technical reports, and score histories. It retains source identities and citations so readers can inspect candidate benchmarks and their evaluation evidence. Daily discovery draws on 37 sources: 13 direct connectors and 24 first-party research and engineering feeds. The catalog contains 1,283 source records drawn from 4 benchmark catalogs and 12,916 numeric observations on 790 records. We describe collection and retrieval, audit the full catalog, and examine benchmark saturation, adoption trends, and the limits of score comparisons. A worked example walks through a complete prior-art search, showing how to query the catalog and inspect benchmark evidence when designing a new evaluation. We release the web dashboard with a benchmark leaderboard, a Pareto frontier view of score against measured use, saturation and trend views, daily feeds, downloadable evidence, a command-line interface (CLI) for offline queries, and reproducible analysis.

</details>


### [96] [KuaiRP Series Role-playing Models Technical Report](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11127)
*Yipeng Wang,Ziwei Zhang,Jiahui Zhang,Qi Gan,Kai Sheng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出KuaiRP角色扮演模型的完整训练与部署方案，通过多阶段训练在深度领域知识注入、通用智能体能力保持、角色扮演质量和部署成本之间取得平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 角色扮演模型通常需要较强的领域知识和稳定的人设遵循能力，但深度领域适配可能导致灾难性遗忘，使模型的通用智能体能力下降。本文旨在减少提示词工程依赖、提升输出稳定性、内置领域世界知识，并实现小参数规模下的高效部署。

Method: 方法包括三个阶段：首先，设计标准化角色模板，并结合用户行为模拟与反向画像过滤构建监督微调数据管线；其次，在强化学习阶段使用基于规则的复合奖励函数，抑制输出变长、重复生成等常见退化现象；最后，提出结合累积散度衰减（CDD）的两阶段在策略蒸馏（OPD）自蒸馏方法，以领域适配模型为教师、原始基础模型为学生，恢复监督微调和强化学习过程中受损的通用能力。

Result: 实验表明，KuaiRP在目标领域的角色扮演保真度方面可达到当前先进专有模型的水平，同时能够恢复通用智能体能力，并保持极低的部署成本。摘要未提供具体数据集、指标数值、参数规模或统计显著性结果。

Conclusion: 多阶段训练管线能够有效缓解领域知识注入与通用能力保持之间的冲突。标准化数据构建、规则化强化学习和带CDD的OPD自蒸馏共同提升了KuaiRP的角色一致性、输出稳定性、通用能力及部署效率，但其跨领域泛化能力和各组件的独立贡献仍需进一步验证。

Abstract: This paper introduces the complete technical solution for the KuaiRP series of role-playing models. We aim to achieve four core objectives for a dedicated role-playing model: simplified prompt engineering, highly stable output quality, built-in domain world knowledge, and high-efficiency deployment with a small parameter size. However, effectively injecting deep domain knowledge often leads to a severe catastrophic forgetting of the model's general agent capabilities. To overcome this trade-off, we propose a multi-stage training pipeline. First, we design a standardized character template and construct an SFT data pipeline based on user behavior simulation and reverse profile filtering. Next, we utilize a rule-based composite reward function during the Reinforcement Learning (RL) phase to eliminate common degradation phenomena like length expansion and repetitive generation. Finally, to recover the general capabilities compromised during SFT and RL, we propose a novel self-distillation paradigm using Two-stage On-Policy Distillation (OPD) equipped with Cumulative-Divergence Decay (CDD). By using the domain-adapted model as the teacher and the original base model as the student, we effectively balance deep domain knowledge injection with the preservation of general agent capabilities. Experimental results demonstrate that the KuaiRP models not only match the current state-of-the-art proprietary models in role-playing fidelity within our target domains, but also successfully recover general agent capabilities, maintaining extremely low deployment costs.

</details>


### [97] [Same Day, Same Story; One Day Ahead, a Different Signal: The Dual Validity of Financial Sentiment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11144)
*AS Aravinthkakshan,Laven Srivastava,Harsh Nandwani*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文检验金融自然语言处理中的语义有效性与预测有效性是否一致。基于证券集体诉讼语料和五种情感分析工具，研究发现，人工标注一致性并不能直接决定市场预测排序，结论显著依赖抽样方式、评分表示和预测时间窗口。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 金融文本研究通常先用人工标签验证情感工具，再假设验证结果能够代表其提取市场信号的能力。该研究质疑这一推断，试图同时评估工具的构念有效性与预测有效性，并分析两者在不同评价设计下的关系。

Method: 研究使用2002年至2025年的证券集体诉讼语料，将约70500条X平台消息与异常股票收益相联系，并以单一标注者构建人工标注金标准。五种工具分别为VADER、Loughran-McDonald、FinBERT、Twitter-RoBERTa和大语言模型标注器。所有工具均通过相同分析流程进行评估，同时比较传统的工具特定抽样与固定样本量面板，并考察同日关联和提前一天预测、连续评分相关性及粗粒度排序。

Result: 在传统的工具特定抽样下，人工一致性与连续评分的同日关联更接近，而不是与提前一天的预测关联更接近。在固定样本量面板中，人工一致性在两个时间窗口上的连续秩相关性相近，但粗粒度类别排序仍然较弱。因此，基准测试的一致性能够支持语义有效性，却不能单独确定工具的预测排序。此外，该对话语料中有17.6%的消息被判定为垃圾信息，消息数量既不能预测市场损害，也不能预测和解金额。

Conclusion: 语义有效性与预测有效性是相关但不同的评价维度，不能将人工标注表现直接视为市场预测能力的代理指标。金融NLP评估应明确抽样规则、评分表示和预测窗口，并同时报告连续关联与离散排序结果。研究还表明，单纯依赖消息数量可能无法捕捉市场损害或诉讼经济结果。

Abstract: Financial NLP has a standard workflow: validate a sentiment tool against human labels, then trust it to extract market signal. This assumes the two evaluations measure the same thing. We test that assumption in a setting where both can be measured at once: a corpus of securities class actions (2002-2025) linking 70,500 X messages to abnormal stock returns, with a single-annotator human labelled gold sample. Running five instruments (VADER, Loughran-McDonald, FinBERT, Twitter-RoBERTa, and an LLM annotator) through one identical pipeline, we find that the relationship between construct and predictive validity depends on the sampling convention and score representation. Under conventional method-specific sampling, human agreement aligns more closely with graded same-day associations than with one-day leads. On a fixed-n panel, however, agreement has similar graded rank correlations at both horizons, while the coarse ordering remains weak. Benchmark agreement therefore establishes semantic validity but does not by itself determine predictive rankings. In a conversation that is 17.6% spam, message volume predicts neither market damage nor settlement size.

</details>


### [98] [The Oligarch Barely Steers Model Collapse in Multi-Model Ecosystems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11146)
*Yangze Liu,Zhongyi Han*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文研究多模型递归训练中的市场集中度是否会加速模型坍塌或使系统向主导模型偏移。实验显示，在所测试范围内，份额分配的不均衡对坍塌速度和最终状态影响很小；决定坍塌速度的关键是参与提供训练文本的模型成员及其对递归训练的易感性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实生成式人工智能生态并非均匀竞争，而可能由少数模型占据大部分文本供给。作者检验两项担忧：第一，文本来源集中、同质化会不会加速递归训练导致的模型坍塌；第二，主导模型能否凭借高市场份额将后续模型持续拉向自身输出分布。

Method: 作者构建受控的多模型生态系统，使用13个参数规模约1B至4B的开放模型，形成3至13个参与者的不同生态。每一代中，各模型生成的文本按其市场份额混入共享语料池，所有模型再从干净的基础权重重新训练；该过程重复5代。实验还注入探针模型，将最高份额推至90%，并施加强偏置；比较不同成员构成、份额配置和人类文本掺混比例下的漂移与坍塌程度。

Result: 提高市场份额不均衡度几乎未改变坍塌速度，且不同份额与主导者身份造成的五代终点差异，仅占各实验共同漂移的一小部分。即使90%高份额与最强注入偏置结合，也不能稳定地将生态系统引导至特定方向；产生的主题变化在坍塌指标上痕迹很弱。相反，在固定份额条件下，仅替换由3个模型组成生态的成员，就能使五代漂移相差2.8倍。基于成员递归训练易感性的份额加权指标可解释19个实验分支中速度差异的68%方差（R²=0.68）。将语料池的一半替换为人类文本，大致可将漂移减半，但不改变其总体方向。

Conclusion: 在该论文覆盖的模型规模、生态结构和五代递归训练范围内，市场集中度既不是模型坍塌速度的主要决定因素，也难以决定坍塌终点或实现稳定操控。更重要的是训练池由哪些模型供给，以及这些供给者自身在递归训练下被带偏、退化的敏感程度。人类文本可有效降低退化幅度，但未显示出改变退化轨迹的作用。

Abstract: AI-generated text is flowing back into the training corpora of the next generation of models. Recursive training on it drives model collapse, and recent work extends the setting to many models feeding one another -- but almost always with the market split evenly, while real generative AI is an oligopoly. Concentration raises two worries: fewer, more uniform sources may make collapse faster, and later models may be dragged toward the oligarch's output. We test both in controlled ecosystems: 13 open 1--4B models form natural ecosystems of 3 to 13 players, plus an injected probe that pushes the top share to 90%; each generation, every model's output is mixed into a shared pool by market share and every model is retrained on that pool from clean base weights, for five generations. Yet within the range we test, neither worry materializes; what emerges instead is an invariance. Making the split more unequal barely changes the speed of collapse. Destinations move even less: the share and identity knobs shift five-generation endpoints by only a few percent of the drift common to all arms -- the ecosystems collapse to nearly the same place. An extreme share paired with the strongest injected bias still does not guarantee steering, and the topic shifts it does produce leave only a faint trace on the ruler that measures collapse. What sets the speed is who supplies the pool and how readily those suppliers are carried along: with every share held fixed, swapping the members of a K=3 ecosystem changes five-generation drift by 2.8x; a share-weighted index of each member's susceptibility explains the speed differences across nineteen arms with R^2 = 0.68; and replacing half the pool with human text roughly halves drift without changing its course. Within the tested range, concentration sets neither the destination nor the pace of collapse; the pace follows whose text fills the pool.

</details>


### [99] [Autonomous Chemical Mechanistic Discovery through Agentic Reasoning and Validation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11147)
*Dong Li,Sixuan Mi,Zihao Ye,Huan Xiong,Tao XU,Tong Zhu,Aijia Zhang,Junqi Gao,Kaiyan Zhang,Shijie Wang,Bowen Zhou,Yuqiang Li,Biqing Qi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出ARCHE，一种结合通用推理模型、化学计算专用模型和结构化工具注册表的自主智能体系统，用于自动化反应机理研究。系统能够生成并筛选机理假设、编排计算流程，并依据计算证据迭代修正结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 反应机理解析对现代化学研究至关重要，但现有计算工作流高度依赖专家介入，难以规模化、自动化和持续验证。因此，研究旨在构建能够自主理解科学问题、提出机理假设并通过计算证据验证和修正结论的闭环系统。

Method: ARCHE整合通用推理模型、领域化学计算模型及结构化工具注册表。系统首先解析研究问题，随后生成和排序候选机理假设，自动调用相应计算工具开展工作流，并在获得计算结果后反复更新假设与结论。作者通过三个递进场景评估系统：重建不对称催化反应的立体控制过渡态并验证机理；通过迭代假设修正提出并验证α-碘硼酸酯C-I键断裂反应的自由基路径；识别控制镍催化迁移交叉偶联反应选择性的可解释化学描述符。

Result: ARCHE在三类任务中展示了自主机理发现能力，包括重建已报道反应的立体控制因素、为新近发现的反应提出可信的自由基机理，以及发现与选择性相关的化学描述符。结果表明，智能体推理与严谨计算验证相结合，可以支持机理假设的生成、筛选和迭代验证。摘要未提供定量性能指标、与人工或其他自动化方法的系统对比，也未明确说明各案例中结论的准确率和计算成本。

Conclusion: ARCHE将反应机理研究转化为可扩展、具有自验证能力的自主闭环流程，为机器辅助化学研究提供了基础。其主要贡献在于工作流编排、假设驱动推理和计算验证的整合；但系统的普适性、可靠性、资源消耗及对复杂未知化学体系的表现仍需通过更广泛的基准和定量评估加以验证。

Abstract: Unraveling reaction mechanisms is central to modern chemistry, yet automating these investigations remains challenging because computational workflows still rely heavily on expert intervention. Here we introduce ARCHE, an autonomous agentic system that integrates a general-purpose reasoning model, a domain-specialized computational chemistry model, and a structured tool registry to transform mechanistic inquiry into a scalable, self-validating process. ARCHE interprets scientific questions, generates and prioritizes mechanistic hypotheses, orchestrates computational workflows, and iteratively refines conclusions based on computed evidence within a closed loop. We validate its capabilities across three increasingly demanding scenarios: reconstructing stereocontrolling transition states and validating the corresponding reaction mechanism in a previously reported asymmetric catalytic reaction; proposing and validating a plausible radical pathway through iterative hypothesis refinement for a recently discovered but unpublished $α$-iodoboronate C-I cleavage reaction; and identifying a chemically interpretable descriptor that governs selectivity in nickel-catalysed migratory cross-coupling reactions. By coupling agentic reasoning with rigorous computational validation, ARCHE advances autonomous mechanistic discovery and establishes a foundation for broader machine-assisted chemical research. The code for ARCHE is publicly available at https://github.com/JetAstra/Arche-Harness.

</details>


### [100] [DRG-MAPPO: Hierarchical Dynamic Role-Graph Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning for Cooperative Air Combat](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11155)
*Junlin Liu,Chengwei Li,Yang Gao,Hui Chang,Xinchen Zhang,Zhijun Zhao,Hao Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.

Method: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.

Result: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.

Conclusion: This content has not passed the compliance test and has been hidden.

Abstract: Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning (MARL) has emerged as a pivotal paradigm for complex decision-making in autonomous systems and air combat. While MARL has demonstrated significant potential in air combat, achieving sophisticated tactical coordination remains a non-trivial challenge. This difficulty is largely attributed to two primary limitations: (1) the absence of structured relational modeling hinders agents from capturing complex, time-varying interactions among battlefield entities; and (2) conventional flat architectures often lack the capability to explicitly model tactical roles, leading to ambiguous task allocation in highly dynamic environments.
